                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(m_boneSearch) &&
                    boneName.IndexOf(m_boneSearch, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) < 0 &&
                    path.IndexOf(m_boneSearch, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) < 0)
                    continue;

                using (new EditorGUILayout.HorizontalScope())
                {
                    bool selected = GUILayout.Toggle(m_selectedBone == i, boneName,
                        "Button", GUILayout.Height(20f));
                    if (selected && m_selectedBone != i)
                    {
                        m_selectedBone = i;
                        UpdateCoverage();
                        SceneView.RepaintAll();
                    }
                    if (GUILayout.Button("◎", GUILayout.Width(28f)))
                        FocusBone(i);
                }
            }
            EditorGUILayout.EndScrollView();
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Weighted Gaussians", m_affectedSplats.ToString("N0"));

            using (new EditorGUILayout.HorizontalScope())
            {
                if (GUILayout.Button("Select Bone"))
                    FocusBone(m_selectedBone);
                using (new EditorGUI.DisabledScope(Application.isPlaying))
                {
                    if (GUILayout.Button("Delete Bone"))
                        DeleteSelectedBone();
                }
            }
        }

        void DrawPaintSection()
        {
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Weight Paint", EditorStyles.boldLabel);
            bool canPaint = RigAsset && RigAsset.BoneCount > 1 && !Application.isPlaying;
            using (new EditorGUI.DisabledScope(!canPaint))
            {
                m_paintEnabled = GUILayout.Toggle(m_paintEnabled,
                    m_paintEnabled ? "Weight Paint Active" : "Enter Weight Paint",
                    "Button", GUILayout.Height(30f));
                m_paintMode = (PaintMode)EditorGUILayout.EnumPopup("Blend", m_paintMode);
                m_brushRadius = EditorGUILayout.Slider("Radius", m_brushRadius, 4f, 200f);
                m_strength = EditorGUILayout.Slider("Strength", m_strength, 0.01f, 1f);
                using (new EditorGUI.DisabledScope(m_paintMode != PaintMode.Draw))
                    m_targetWeight = EditorGUILayout.Slider("Weight", m_targetWeight, 0f, 1f);
                m_xray = EditorGUILayout.ToggleLeft("X-Ray (paint through the splat)", m_xray);
                m_showWeightOverlay = EditorGUILayout.ToggleLeft(
                    "Show selected-bone weights", m_showWeightOverlay);

                if (GUILayout.Button("Normalize All Weights"))
                {
                    Undo.RegisterCompleteObjectUndo(RigAsset, "Normalize Gaussian Weights");
                    RigAsset.NormalizeAll();
                    CommitAssetChange("Normalized all Gaussian weights.");
                }
            }

            if (Application.isPlaying)
                EditorGUILayout.HelpBox("Exit Play mode before editing the rig.", MessageType.Warning);
            else
                EditorGUILayout.HelpBox(
                    "Paint in the Scene view. Shift subtracts; Ctrl/Cmd smooths. " +
                    "Use [ and ] to resize the brush. Weight colors run blue → cyan → yellow → red.",
                    MessageType.None);
        }

        void CreateArmatureRoot()
        {
            GsplatRig rig = Rig;
            if (!rig || !m_renderer)
                return;
            var rootObject = new GameObject("Gaussian Armature");
            Undo.RegisterCreatedObjectUndo(rootObject, "Create Gaussian Armature");
            Undo.SetTransformParent(rootObject.transform, m_renderer.transform,
                "Parent Gaussian Armature");
            rootObject.transform.localPosition = Vector3.zero;
            rootObject.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
            rootObject.transform.localScale = Vector3.one;
            Undo.RecordObject(rig, "Assign Gaussian Armature");
            rig.ArmatureRoot = rootObject.transform;
            rig.RebuildBindings();
            EditorUtility.SetDirty(rig);
            Undo.RecordObject(m_renderer, "Use Manual Gaussian Rig");
            m_renderer.DeformationSource = GsplatRenderer.GsplatDeformationSource.ManualRig;
            EditorUtility.SetDirty(m_renderer);
            Selection.activeGameObject = rootObject;
            m_status = "Armature created. Add a root bone, then create the rig asset.";
            SceneView.RepaintAll();
        }

        bool CanCreateRig(out string reason)
        {
            if (!m_renderer || !m_renderer.GsplatAsset)
                reason = "Assign a Gaussian renderer and asset.";
            else if (!Rig || !Rig.ArmatureRoot)
                reason = "Create or assign an Armature Root.";
            else
            {
                reason = null;
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        static Transform FindSuggestedProxyRoot(GsplatRenderer renderer)
        {
            if (!renderer)
                return null;
            Renderer proxyRenderer = renderer.GetComponentInChildren<SkinnedMeshRenderer>(true);
            if (!proxyRenderer)
                proxyRenderer = renderer.GetComponentInChildren<MeshRenderer>(true);
            if (!proxyRenderer)
                return null;
            Transform candidate = proxyRenderer.transform;
            while (candidate.parent && candidate.parent != renderer.transform)
                candidate = candidate.parent;
            return candidate;
        }

        void CreateRigAsset()
        {
            if (!CanCreateRig(out string reason))
            {
                EditorUtility.DisplayDialog("Gaussian Rigging", reason, "OK");
                return;
            }

            string defaultName = $"{m_renderer.GsplatAsset.name} Rig.asset";
            string path = EditorUtility.SaveFilePanelInProject("Save Gaussian Rig",
                defaultName, "asset", "Choose where the painted Gaussian rig will be saved.");
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
                return;

            var asset = CreateInstance<GsplatRigAsset>();
            asset.name = System.IO.Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(path);
            AssetDatabase.CreateAsset(asset, path);
            Undo.RegisterCreatedObjectUndo(asset, "Create Gaussian Rig");
            Undo.RecordObject(Rig, "Assign Gaussian Rig");
            Rig.RigAsset = asset;
            Undo.RecordObject(m_renderer, "Use Manual Gaussian Rig");
            m_renderer.DeformationSource = GsplatRenderer.GsplatDeformationSource.ManualRig;
            EditorUtility.SetDirty(m_renderer);
            SynchronizeRigAsset(true, $"Created '{asset.name}'. Add or select a bone and paint its weights.");
            Selection.activeGameObject = m_renderer.gameObject;
        }

        void SynchronizeRigAsset(bool updateBindPose, string status)
        {
            GsplatRig rig = Rig;
            GsplatRigAsset asset = RigAsset;
            if (!rig || !asset || !rig.ArmatureRoot || !m_renderer.GsplatAsset)
                return;

            Undo.RegisterCompleteObjectUndo(asset,
                updateBindPose ? "Set Gaussian Bind Pose" : "Sync Gaussian Bones");

            string[] oldPaths = asset.CopyBonePaths();
            Matrix4x4[] oldBind = asset.CopyBindTransforms();
            GsplatRigWeight[] oldWeights = asset.CopyWeights();
            var oldIndexByPath = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            for (int i = 1; i < oldPaths.Length; ++i)
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(oldPaths[i]))
                    oldIndexByPath[oldPaths[i]] = i;

            Transform[] bones = GetArmatureBones(rig.ArmatureRoot);
            var paths = new string[bones.Length + 1];
            var names = new string[bones.Length + 1];
            var bind = new Matrix4x4[bones.Length + 1];
            paths[0] = string.Empty;
            names[0] = "Unassigned";
            bind[0] = Matrix4x4.identity;
            var newIndexByPath = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            for (int i = 0; i < bones.Length; ++i)
            {
                int paletteIndex = i + 1;
                string bonePath = GetRelativePath(rig.ArmatureRoot, bones[i]);
                paths[paletteIndex] = bonePath;
                names[paletteIndex] = bones[i].name;
                newIndexByPath[bonePath] = paletteIndex;
                if (!updateBindPose && oldIndexByPath.TryGetValue(bonePath, out int oldIndex) &&
                    oldIndex < oldBind.Length)
                    bind[paletteIndex] = oldBind[oldIndex];
                else
                    bind[paletteIndex] = bones[i].worldToLocalMatrix *
                                         m_renderer.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
            }

            int splatCount = checked((int)m_renderer.GsplatAsset.SplatCount);
            var weights = new GsplatRigWeight[splatCount];
            bool preserve = oldWeights.Length == splatCount && oldPaths.Length > 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < splatCount; ++i)
                weights[i] = preserve
                    ? RemapWeight(oldWeights[i], oldPaths, newIndexByPath)
                    : new GsplatRigWeight { BoneIndex0 = 0, Weight0 = 1f };

            asset.SetData(m_renderer.GsplatAsset, paths, names, bind, weights);
            EditorUtility.SetDirty(asset);
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssetIfDirty(asset);
            rig.RebuildBindings();
            EditorUtility.SetDirty(rig);
            ClampSelectedBone();
            RefreshCenters();
            UpdateCoverage();
            m_status = status;
            SceneView.RepaintAll();
            Repaint();
        }

        void SetCurrentBindPose()
        {
            GsplatRig rig = Rig;
            GsplatRigAsset asset = RigAsset;
            if (!rig || !asset || !rig.ArmatureRoot || !m_renderer.GsplatAsset)
                return;

            string[] paths = asset.CopyBonePaths();
            string[] names = asset.CopyBoneNames();
            Matrix4x4[] bind = asset.CopyBindTransforms();
            GsplatRigWeight[] weights = asset.CopyWeights();
            if (paths.Length != bind.Length)
                return;

            Undo.RegisterCompleteObjectUndo(asset, "Set Gaussian Bind Pose");
            bind[0] = Matrix4x4.identity;
            for (int i = 1; i < paths.Length; ++i)
            {
                Transform bone = rig.ArmatureRoot.Find(paths[i]);
                if (!bone)
                {
                    EditorUtility.DisplayDialog("Gaussian Bind Pose",
                        $"Bone '{paths[i]}' cannot be found under '{rig.ArmatureRoot.name}'.", "OK");
                    return;
                }
                bind[i] = bone.worldToLocalMatrix * m_renderer.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
            }

            asset.SetData(m_renderer.GsplatAsset, paths, names, bind, weights);
            EditorUtility.SetDirty(asset);
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssetIfDirty(asset);
            rig.RebuildBindings();
            m_status = "Stored the current skeleton pose as the Gaussian bind pose.";
            SceneView.RepaintAll();
            Repaint();
        }

        static GsplatRigWeight RemapWeight(GsplatRigWeight source, string[] oldPaths,
            Dictionary<string, int> newIndexByPath)
        {
            int Remap(int oldIndex)
            {
                if (oldIndex <= 0 || oldIndex >= oldPaths.Length)
                    return 0;
                return newIndexByPath.TryGetValue(oldPaths[oldIndex], out int newIndex)
                    ? newIndex
                    : 0;
            }

            return GsplatRigWeight.FromInfluences(
                Remap(source.BoneIndex0), source.Weight0,
                Remap(source.BoneIndex1), source.Weight1,
                Remap(source.BoneIndex2), source.Weight2,
                Remap(source.BoneIndex3), source.Weight3);
        }

        static Transform[] GetArmatureBones(Transform root)
        {
            if (!root)
                return Array.Empty<Transform>();
            Transform[] hierarchy = root.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true);
            if (hierarchy.Length <= 1)
                return Array.Empty<Transform>();
            var bones = new Transform[hierarchy.Length - 1];
            Array.Copy(hierarchy, 1, bones, 0, bones.Length);
            return bones;
        }

        static string GetRelativePath(Transform root, Transform child)
        {
            var parts = new List<string>();
            for (Transform current = child; current && current != root; current = current.parent)
                parts.Add(current.name);
            parts.Reverse();
            return string.Join("/", parts);
        }

        void BeginBonePlacement(Transform parent)
        {
            if (!parent || !m_renderer || !m_renderer.GsplatAsset)
                return;
            m_paintEnabled = false;
            m_placingBone = true;
            m_placementParent = parent;
            RefreshCenters();
            m_status = parent == Rig.ArmatureRoot
                ? "Click a Gaussian to place a root bone."
                : $"Click a Gaussian to extrude a child from '{parent.name}'.";
            UpdateInteractionState();
            SceneView.RepaintAll();
        }

        void PlaceBone(Vector3 worldPosition)
        {
            if (!m_placementParent || !Rig)
                return;
            string boneName = GameObjectUtility.GetUniqueNameForSibling(
                m_placementParent, "Bone");
            var boneObject = new GameObject(boneName);
            Undo.RegisterCreatedObjectUndo(boneObject, "Add Gaussian Bone");
            Undo.SetTransformParent(boneObject.transform, m_placementParent,
                "Parent Gaussian Bone");
            boneObject.transform.position = worldPosition;
            boneObject.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
            boneObject.transform.localScale = Vector3.one;
            m_placingBone = false;
            m_placementParent = null;

            if (RigAsset)
            {
                SynchronizeRigAsset(false, $"Added bone '{boneName}'.");
                string path = GetRelativePath(Rig.ArmatureRoot, boneObject.transform);
                for (int i = 1; i < RigAsset.BoneCount; ++i)
                    if (RigAsset.BonePaths[i] == path)
                    {
                        m_selectedBone = i;
                        break;
                    }
            }
            else
                m_status = $"Added bone '{boneName}'. Create a rig asset to make it paintable.";

            Selection.activeGameObject = boneObject;
            UpdateCoverage();
            UpdateInteractionState();
            SceneView.RepaintAll();
            Repaint();
        }

        void DeleteSelectedBone()
        {
            Transform bone = Rig?.ResolveRigBone(m_selectedBone);
            if (!bone)
                return;
            if (!EditorUtility.DisplayDialog("Delete Gaussian Bone",
                    $"Delete '{bone.name}' and all of its child bones? Their weights will become unassigned.",
                    "Delete", "Cancel"))
                return;
            string boneName = bone.name;
            Undo.DestroyObjectImmediate(bone.gameObject);
            SynchronizeRigAsset(false, $"Deleted bone '{boneName}'.");
        }

        void FocusBone(int boneIndex)
        {
            Transform bone = Rig?.ResolveRigBone(boneIndex);
            if (!bone)
                return;
            Selection.activeTransform = bone;
            SceneView.lastActiveSceneView?.FrameSelected();
        }

        void ClampSelectedBone()
        {
            GsplatRigAsset asset = RigAsset;
            m_selectedBone = asset && asset.BoneCount > 1
                ? Mathf.Clamp(m_selectedBone, 1, asset.BoneCount - 1)
                : 1;
        }

        void DuringSceneGui(SceneView sceneView)
        {
            UpdateInteractionState();
            if (!m_renderer || !sceneView || !sceneView.camera)
                return;

            RefreshCenters();
            Event current = Event.current;
            if (current == null)
                return;

            int controlId = GUIUtility.GetControlID(k_sceneControlHash, FocusType.Passive);
            bool interacting = m_placingBone || IsPaintReady();
            if (current.type == EventType.Layout && interacting && !current.alt)
                HandleUtility.AddDefaultControl(controlId);

            if (current.type == EventType.Repaint)
            {
                DrawArmatureOverlay();
                if (m_placingBone)
                {
                    if (TryFindNearestSplat(sceneView.camera, current.mousePosition,
                            out Vector3 placement))
                        DrawPlacementPreview(placement);
                }
                else if (IsPaintReady())
                    DrawPaintOverlay(sceneView, current.mousePosition);
            }

            if (m_placingBone)
            {
                if (current.type == EventType.KeyDown && current.keyCode == KeyCode.Escape)
                {
                    m_placingBone = false;
                    m_placementParent = null;
                    m_status = "Bone placement cancelled.";
                    current.Use();
                    UpdateInteractionState();
                    Repaint();
                }
                else if (!current.alt && current.button == 0 && current.type == EventType.MouseDown &&
                         TryFindNearestSplat(sceneView.camera, current.mousePosition,
                             out Vector3 placement))
                {
                    PlaceBone(placement);
                    current.Use();
                }
                return;
            }

            if (!IsPaintReady())
                return;
            HandleBrushShortcuts(current);
            if (current.alt || current.button != 0)
                return;
            if (current.type == EventType.MouseDown)
            {
                GUIUtility.hotControl = controlId;
                m_strokeActive = true;
                Undo.RegisterCompleteObjectUndo(RigAsset, "Paint Gaussian Weights");
                PaintAt(sceneView, current.mousePosition, current);
                current.Use();
            }
            else if (current.type == EventType.MouseDrag &&
                     m_strokeActive && GUIUtility.hotControl == controlId)
            {
                PaintAt(sceneView, current.mousePosition, current);
                current.Use();
            }
            else if (current.type == EventType.MouseUp && m_strokeActive)
            {
                m_strokeActive = false;
                if (GUIUtility.hotControl == controlId)
                    GUIUtility.hotControl = 0;
                CommitAssetChange("Painted Gaussian weights.");
                current.Use();
            }
        }

        bool TryFindNearestSplat(Camera camera, Vector2 mousePosition, out Vector3 worldPosition)
        {
            worldPosition = default;
            if (m_centers.Length == 0)
                return false;
            float bestDistance = float.PositiveInfinity;
            Matrix4x4 localToWorld = m_renderer.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
            for (int i = 0; i < m_centers.Length; ++i)
            {
                Vector3 world = localToWorld.MultiplyPoint3x4(m_centers[i]);
                if (camera.WorldToViewportPoint(world).z <= 0f)
                    continue;
                float distance = (HandleUtility.WorldToGUIPoint(world) - mousePosition).sqrMagnitude;
                if (distance >= bestDistance)
                    continue;
                bestDistance = distance;
                worldPosition = world;
            }
            return bestDistance <= 35f * 35f;
        }

        static void DrawPlacementPreview(Vector3 position)
        {
            Handles.color = new Color(0.2f, 1f, 0.35f, 1f);
            float size = HandleUtility.GetHandleSize(position) * 0.12f;
            Handles.SphereHandleCap(0, position, Quaternion.identity, size, EventType.Repaint);
        }

        void DrawArmatureOverlay()
        {
            GsplatRig rig = Rig;
            if (!rig || !rig.ArmatureRoot)
                return;
            Transform selected = rig.ResolveRigBone(m_selectedBone);
            var displayedBones = new List<Transform>();
            if (RigAsset && RigAsset.BoneCount > 1)
            {
                for (int i = 1; i < RigAsset.BoneCount; ++i)
                {
                    Transform bone = rig.ResolveRigBone(i);
                    if (bone)
                        displayedBones.Add(bone);
                }
            }
            else
                displayedBones.AddRange(GetArmatureBones(rig.ArmatureRoot));

            foreach (Transform bone in displayedBones)
            {
                bool isSelected = bone == selected;
                Handles.color = isSelected
                    ? new Color(1f, 0.55f, 0.05f, 1f)
                    : new Color(0.95f, 0.72f, 0.25f, 0.7f);
                float size = HandleUtility.GetHandleSize(bone.position) * (isSelected ? 0.09f : 0.06f);
                Handles.SphereHandleCap(0, bone.position, Quaternion.identity, size,
                    EventType.Repaint);
                if (bone.parent && bone.parent != rig.ArmatureRoot)
                    Handles.DrawAAPolyLine(isSelected ? 4f : 2f,
                        bone.position, bone.parent.position);
                if (isSelected)
                    Handles.Label(bone.position, $"  {bone.name}");
            }

            if (m_placingBone && m_placementParent && m_placementParent != rig.ArmatureRoot)
            {
                Handles.color = new Color(0.2f, 1f, 0.35f, 0.85f);
                Handles.Label(m_placementParent.position, $"  Extruding from {m_placementParent.name}");
            }
        }

        void HandleBrushShortcuts(Event current)
        {
            if (current.type != EventType.KeyDown)
                return;
            if (current.keyCode == KeyCode.LeftBracket)
            {
                m_brushRadius = Mathf.Max(4f, m_brushRadius - 5f);
                current.Use();
                Repaint();
            }
            else if (current.keyCode == KeyCode.RightBracket)
            {
                m_brushRadius = Mathf.Min(200f, m_brushRadius + 5f);
                current.Use();
                Repaint();
            }
        }

        void PaintAt(SceneView sceneView, Vector2 mousePosition, Event current)
        {
            CollectBrushHits(sceneView.camera, mousePosition);
            if (m_brushHits.Count == 0)
                return;

            PaintMode mode = current.control || current.command
                ? PaintMode.Smooth
                : current.shift ? PaintMode.Subtract : m_paintMode;
            float smoothTarget = 0f;
            if (mode == PaintMode.Smooth)
            {
                foreach (BrushHit hit in m_brushHits)
                    smoothTarget += RigAsset.GetBoneWeight(hit.Index, m_selectedBone);
                smoothTarget /= m_brushHits.Count;
            }

            int changed = 0;
            foreach (BrushHit hit in m_brushHits)
            {
                float falloff = 1f - Mathf.SmoothStep(0f, 1f, hit.Radius01);
                float influence = Mathf.Clamp01(m_strength * falloff);
                float currentWeight = RigAsset.GetBoneWeight(hit.Index, m_selectedBone);
                float next = mode switch
                {
                    PaintMode.Draw => Mathf.Lerp(currentWeight, m_targetWeight, influence),
                    PaintMode.Add => currentWeight + (1f - currentWeight) * influence,
                    PaintMode.Subtract => currentWeight * (1f - influence),
                    PaintMode.Smooth => Mathf.Lerp(currentWeight, smoothTarget, influence),
                    _ => currentWeight
                };
                if (Mathf.Abs(next - currentWeight) <= 1e-5f)
                    continue;
                RigAsset.SetBoneWeight(hit.Index, m_selectedBone, next, true);
                changed++;
            }
            if (changed == 0)
                return;
            EditorUtility.SetDirty(RigAsset);
            m_status = $"Painted {changed:N0} Gaussians ({mode}).";
            sceneView.Repaint();
            Repaint();
        }

        void CollectBrushHits(Camera camera, Vector2 mousePosition)
        {
            m_brushHits.Clear();
            m_nearestDepthByCell.Clear();
            float radiusSquared = m_brushRadius * m_brushRadius;
            Matrix4x4 localToWorld = m_renderer.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
            for (int i = 0; i < m_centers.Length; ++i)
            {
                Vector3 world = localToWorld.MultiplyPoint3x4(m_centers[i]);
                Vector3 viewport = camera.WorldToViewportPoint(world);
                if (viewport.z <= 0f)
                    continue;
                Vector2 gui = HandleUtility.WorldToGUIPoint(world);
                float distanceSquared = (gui - mousePosition).sqrMagnitude;
                if (distanceSquared > radiusSquared)
                    continue;
                int cellX = Mathf.FloorToInt(gui.x / 6f);
                int cellY = Mathf.FloorToInt(gui.y / 6f);
                long cell = ((long)cellX << 32) ^ (uint)cellY;
                float radius01 = Mathf.Sqrt(distanceSquared) / m_brushRadius;
                m_brushHits.Add(new BrushHit(i, radius01, viewport.z, cell));
                if (!m_nearestDepthByCell.TryGetValue(cell, out float depth) || viewport.z < depth)
                    m_nearestDepthByCell[cell] = viewport.z;
            }

            if (m_xray || m_brushHits.Count == 0)
                return;
            float worldDiagonal = m_renderer.transform.TransformVector(
                m_renderer.GsplatAsset.Bounds.size).magnitude;
            float depthTolerance = Mathf.Max(0.0001f, worldDiagonal * k_frontDepthFraction);
            int write = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < m_brushHits.Count; ++i)
            {
                BrushHit hit = m_brushHits[i];
                if (hit.Depth > m_nearestDepthByCell[hit.Cell] + depthTolerance)
                    continue;
                m_brushHits[write++] = hit;
            }
            if (write < m_brushHits.Count)
                m_brushHits.RemoveRange(write, m_brushHits.Count - write);
        }

        void DrawPaintOverlay(SceneView sceneView, Vector2 mousePosition)
        {
            GsplatRigAsset asset = RigAsset;
            Handles.BeginGUI();
            if (m_showWeightOverlay)
            {
                int step = Mathf.Max(1, m_centers.Length / k_overlayPointLimit);
                Matrix4x4 localToWorld = m_renderer.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
                for (int i = 0; i < m_centers.Length; i += step)
                {
                    float weight = asset.GetBoneWeight(i, m_selectedBone);
                    Vector3 world = localToWorld.MultiplyPoint3x4(m_centers[i]);
                    if (sceneView.camera.WorldToViewportPoint(world).z <= 0f)
                        continue;
                    Vector2 gui = HandleUtility.WorldToGUIPoint(world);
                    EditorGUI.DrawRect(new Rect(gui.x - 1.5f, gui.y - 1.5f, 3f, 3f),
                        WeightColor(weight));
                }
            }

            var circle = new Vector3[49];
            for (int i = 0; i < circle.Length; ++i)
            {
                float angle = i * Mathf.PI * 2f / (circle.Length - 1);
                circle[i] = mousePosition + new Vector2(Mathf.Cos(angle), Mathf.Sin(angle)) *
                    m_brushRadius;
            }
            Handles.color = Color.white;
            Handles.DrawAAPolyLine(2f, circle);
            Handles.EndGUI();
        }

        static Color WeightColor(float weight)
        {
            weight = Mathf.Clamp01(weight);
            if (weight < 0.33f)
                return Color.Lerp(new Color(0.05f, 0.1f, 0.8f), Color.cyan, weight / 0.33f);
            if (weight < 0.66f)
                return Color.Lerp(Color.cyan, Color.yellow, (weight - 0.33f) / 0.33f);
            return Color.Lerp(Color.yellow, Color.red, (weight - 0.66f) / 0.34f);
        }

        void RefreshCenters()
        {
            if (!m_renderer || !m_renderer.GsplatAsset)
            {
                m_cachedCenterAsset = null;
                m_centers = Array.Empty<Vector3>();
                return;
            }
            if (m_cachedCenterAsset == m_renderer.GsplatAsset &&
                m_centers.Length == m_renderer.GsplatAsset.SplatCount)
                return;
            m_cachedCenterAsset = m_renderer.GsplatAsset;
            try
            {
                m_centers = GsplatRig.GetSplatCenters(m_cachedCenterAsset);
            }
            catch (Exception exception)
            {
                m_centers = Array.Empty<Vector3>();
                m_status = exception.Message;
            }
        }

        void UpdateCoverage()
        {
            GsplatRigAsset asset = RigAsset;
            if (!asset || m_selectedBone <= 0 || m_selectedBone >= asset.BoneCount)
            {
                m_affectedSplats = 0;
                return;
            }
            int count = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < asset.SplatCount; ++i)
                if (asset.GetBoneWeight(i, m_selectedBone) > 0.001f)
                    count++;
            m_affectedSplats = count;
        }

        void CommitAssetChange(string status)
        {
            if (!RigAsset)
                return;
            EditorUtility.SetDirty(RigAsset);
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssetIfDirty(RigAsset);
            Rig?.RebuildBindings();
            UpdateCoverage();
            m_status = status;
            SceneView.RepaintAll();
            Repaint();
        }

        void OnUndoRedo()
        {
            Rig?.RebuildBindings();
            ClampSelectedBone();
            UpdateCoverage();
            SceneView.RepaintAll();
            Repaint();
        }

        bool IsPaintReady() => m_paintEnabled && !Application.isPlaying &&
                               m_renderer && RigAsset && m_selectedBone > 0 &&
                               m_selectedBone < RigAsset.BoneCount && m_centers.Length > 0;

        void UpdateInteractionState()
        {
            if (Application.isPlaying || !RigAsset || RigAsset.BoneCount <= 1)
                m_paintEnabled = false;
            IsSceneRiggingActive = m_placingBone || IsPaintReady();
        }
    }
}
```

### Editor/GsplatScenePicker.cs
```
// Copyright (c) 2026 ARLOOPA
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Gsplat.Editor
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Adds bounds-based GsplatRenderer hits to Unity's native Scene-view picker. Unity keeps
    /// ownership of click, cycling, modifier, hierarchy-filter, and ignore-list behavior.
    /// </summary>
    [InitializeOnLoad]
    static class GsplatScenePicker
    {
        const float k_directionEpsilon = 1e-7f;
        const int k_pickerControlHash = 0x4753504C; // 'GSPL'
        const float k_boundsControlDistance = 1f;
        const float k_selectionBoundsPadding = 0.02f;
        const float k_minSelectionBoundsPadding = 1e-4f;
        static readonly int k_selectionId = Shader.PropertyToID("_SelectionId");
        static Mesh s_boundsMesh;
        static Material s_pickingMaterial;

        static GsplatScenePicker()
        {
            // Unity 6 normally resolves Scene clicks through its GPU picking texture. Render an
            // editor-only bounds cube into that texture so a splat behaves like ordinary scene
            // geometry without adding a physics collider to the user's GameObject.
            HandleUtility.UnregisterRenderPickingCallback(RenderPickingBounds);
            HandleUtility.RegisterRenderPickingCallback(RenderPickingBounds);

            // Retain the classic PickGameObject callback for selection APIs and editor paths
            // that do not request a render-picking pass.
            HandleUtility.pickGameObjectCustomPasses -= PickGameObject;
            HandleUtility.pickGameObjectCustomPasses += PickGameObject;

            // Unity 6 has several Scene-view selection paths. Some camera angles can resolve
            // procedural splats through a path that never consumes either custom callback.
            // Keep a direct click fallback so bounds selection remains deterministic.
            SceneView.beforeSceneGui -= OnSceneGUI;
            SceneView.beforeSceneGui += OnSceneGUI;
        }

        static void OnSceneGUI(SceneView sceneView)
        {
            if (GsplatRiggingWindow.IsSceneRiggingActive)
                return;
            Event current = Event.current;
            if (current == null || current.alt || !sceneView || !sceneView.camera)
                return;

            int controlId = GUIUtility.GetControlID(k_pickerControlHash, FocusType.Passive);
            Ray ray = HandleUtility.GUIPointToWorldRay(current.mousePosition);
            GsplatRenderer renderer = FindClosestBounds(ray, sceneView.camera.cullingMask, null, null);
            if (!renderer)
                return;

            if (current.type == EventType.Layout)
            {
                // A transform handle normally reports distance 0 and therefore wins. The bounds
                // control wins over empty-scene picking while leaving active editor tools usable.
                HandleUtility.AddControl(controlId, k_boundsControlDistance);
                return;
            }

            if (current.type != EventType.MouseDown || current.button != 0 ||
                GUIUtility.hotControl != 0 || HandleUtility.nearestControl != controlId)
                return;

            ApplySelection(renderer.gameObject, current.shift, current.control || current.command);
            current.Use();
            sceneView.Repaint();
        }

        static void ApplySelection(GameObject gameObject, bool additive, bool toggle)
        {
            if (!additive && !toggle)
            {
                Selection.activeGameObject = gameObject;
                return;
            }

            var selected = Selection.objects.ToList();
            bool contains = selected.Contains(gameObject);
            if (toggle && contains)
            {
                selected.Remove(gameObject);
                Selection.objects = selected.ToArray();
                return;
            }

            if (!contains)
                selected.Add(gameObject);
            Selection.objects = selected.ToArray();
            Selection.activeGameObject = gameObject;
        }

        static RenderPickingResult RenderPickingBounds(in RenderPickingArgs args)
        {
            EnsurePickingResources();
            if (!s_boundsMesh || !s_pickingMaterial)
                return RenderPickingResult.NoOperation;

            var pickedObjects = new List<GameObject>();
            var renderers = UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<GsplatRenderer>(FindObjectsSortMode.None);
            foreach (var renderer in renderers)
            {
                if (!renderer || !renderer.isActiveAndEnabled || !renderer.GsplatAsset ||
                    !args.NeedToRenderForPicking(renderer.gameObject))
                    continue;

                Bounds bounds = GetPickingBounds(renderer.GsplatAsset);
                if (!IsFinite(bounds.center) || !IsFinite(bounds.size))
                    continue;

                int pickingIndex = args.pickingIndex + pickedObjects.Count;
                s_pickingMaterial.SetVector(k_selectionId, HandleUtility.EncodeSelectionId(pickingIndex));
                if (!s_pickingMaterial.SetPass(0))
                    continue;

                Matrix4x4 boundsTransform = renderer.transform.localToWorldMatrix *
                                            Matrix4x4.TRS(bounds.center, Quaternion.identity, bounds.size);
                Graphics.DrawMeshNow(s_boundsMesh, boundsTransform);
                pickedObjects.Add(renderer.gameObject);
            }

            if (pickedObjects.Count == 0)
                return RenderPickingResult.NoOperation;

            return new RenderPickingResult(pickedObjects.Count, localPickingIndex =>
                localPickingIndex >= 0 && localPickingIndex < pickedObjects.Count
                    ? pickedObjects[localPickingIndex]
                    : null);
        }

        static void EnsurePickingResources()
        {
            if (!s_pickingMaterial)
            {
                Shader shader = Shader.Find("Hidden/Gsplat/EditorBoundsPicking");
                if (shader)
                {
                    s_pickingMaterial = new Material(shader)
                    {
                        name = "Gsplat Editor Bounds Picking",
                        hideFlags = HideFlags.HideAndDontSave
                    };
                }
            }

            if (s_boundsMesh)
                return;

            s_boundsMesh = new Mesh
            {
                name = "Gsplat Editor Bounds Picking Cube",
                hideFlags = HideFlags.HideAndDontSave,
                vertices = new[]
                {
                    new Vector3(-0.5f, -0.5f, -0.5f),
                    new Vector3( 0.5f, -0.5f, -0.5f),
                    new Vector3( 0.5f,  0.5f, -0.5f),
                    new Vector3(-0.5f,  0.5f, -0.5f),
                    new Vector3(-0.5f, -0.5f,  0.5f),
                    new Vector3( 0.5f, -0.5f,  0.5f),
                    new Vector3( 0.5f,  0.5f,  0.5f),
                    new Vector3(-0.5f,  0.5f,  0.5f)
                },
                triangles = new[]
                {
                    0, 2, 1, 0, 3, 2,
                    1, 2, 6, 1, 6, 5,
                    5, 6, 7, 5, 7, 4,
                    4, 7, 3, 4, 3, 0,
                    3, 7, 6, 3, 6, 2,
                    4, 0, 1, 4, 1, 5
                }
            };
            s_boundsMesh.RecalculateBounds();
            s_boundsMesh.UploadMeshData(true);
        }

        static bool IsFinite(Vector3 value) =>
            float.IsFinite(value.x) && float.IsFinite(value.y) && float.IsFinite(value.z);

        static GameObject PickGameObject(Camera camera, int layers, Vector2 position,
            GameObject[] ignore, GameObject[] filter, out int materialIndex)
        {
            materialIndex = -1;
            if (!camera)
                return null;

            // The callback position is in top-left-origin GUI coordinates. Let Unity apply
            // Scene-view toolbar, viewport, and HiDPI offsets exactly as its built-in picker does.
            Ray ray = HandleUtility.GUIPointToWorldRay(position);

            GsplatRenderer closest = FindClosestBounds(ray, layers, ignore, filter);

            return closest ? closest.gameObject : null;
        }

        static GsplatRenderer FindClosestBounds(Ray ray, int layers, GameObject[] ignore, GameObject[] filter)
        {
            GsplatRenderer closest = null;
            float closestDistance = float.PositiveInfinity;
            var renderers = UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<GsplatRenderer>(FindObjectsSortMode.None);
            foreach (var renderer in renderers)
            {
                if (!renderer || !renderer.isActiveAndEnabled || !renderer.GsplatAsset)
                    continue;

                GameObject gameObject = renderer.gameObject;
                if ((layers & (1 << gameObject.layer)) == 0 || Contains(ignore, gameObject) ||
                    (filter != null && !Contains(filter, gameObject)))
                    continue;

                var visibility = SceneVisibilityManager.instance;
                if (visibility != null &&
                    (visibility.IsHidden(gameObject, false) || visibility.IsPickingDisabled(gameObject, false)))
                    continue;

                if (!IntersectLocalBounds(ray, renderer.transform.worldToLocalMatrix,
                        GetPickingBounds(renderer.GsplatAsset), out float distance) || distance >= closestDistance)
                    continue;

                closest = renderer;
                closestDistance = distance;
            }

            return closest;
        }

        static Bounds GetPickingBounds(GsplatAsset asset)
        {
            Bounds bounds = asset.Bounds;
            float largestDimension = Mathf.Max(bounds.size.x, bounds.size.y, bounds.size.z);
            // Asset bounds primarily describe splat centers. Add a small selection-only shell
            // so flat captures and edge Gaussian footprints remain clickable at oblique angles.
            float expansion = Mathf.Max(largestDimension * k_selectionBoundsPadding,
                k_minSelectionBoundsPadding);
            bounds.Expand(expansion);
            return bounds;
        }

        static bool Contains(GameObject[] objects, GameObject target)
        {
            if (objects == null)
                return false;
            return Array.IndexOf(objects, target) >= 0;
        }

        internal static bool IntersectLocalBounds(Ray worldRay, Matrix4x4 worldToLocal,
            Bounds localBounds, out float distance)
        {
            Vector3 origin = worldToLocal.MultiplyPoint3x4(worldRay.origin);
            Vector3 direction = worldToLocal.MultiplyVector(worldRay.direction);
            Vector3 min = localBounds.min;
            Vector3 max = localBounds.max;
            float near = 0f;
            float far = float.PositiveInfinity;

            if (!IntersectAxis(origin.x, direction.x, min.x, max.x, ref near, ref far) ||
                !IntersectAxis(origin.y, direction.y, min.y, max.y, ref near, ref far) ||
                !IntersectAxis(origin.z, direction.z, min.z, max.z, ref near, ref far))
            {
                distance = 0f;
                return false;
            }

            distance = near;
            return far >= 0f;
        }

        static bool IntersectAxis(float origin, float direction, float min, float max,
            ref float near, ref float far)
        {
            if (Mathf.Abs(direction) < k_directionEpsilon)
                return origin >= min && origin <= max;

            float inverse = 1f / direction;
            float first = (min - origin) * inverse;
            float second = (max - origin) * inverse;
            if (first > second)
                (first, second) = (second, first);

            near = Mathf.Max(near, first);
            far = Mathf.Min(far, second);
            return near <= far;
        }
    }
}
```

### Editor/GsplatSceneRelighting.cs
```
// Copyright (c) 2026 ARLOOPA
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;

namespace Gsplat.Editor
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Maintains unsaved relighting captures for Scene-view cameras. Runtime renderers retain
    /// their configured Game-camera source; GsplatRelighting selects this camera-specific map
    /// immediately before each camera renders.
    /// </summary>
    [InitializeOnLoad]
    internal static class GsplatSceneRelighting
    {
        sealed class SceneCameraState
        {
            internal Camera Camera;
            internal GsplatRelighting Relighting;
            internal bool OwnsRelighting;
            internal bool OriginalRenderOnDemand;
            internal int ManagedProxyLayers;
            internal int OriginalProxyLayerBits;
        }

        struct CaptureSettings
        {
            internal int ProxyLayers;
            internal float TextureScale;
            internal float Blend;
            internal float Brightness;
            internal float Background;

            internal bool IsValid => ProxyLayers != 0;
        }

        static readonly Dictionary<Camera, SceneCameraState> s_states = new();
        static CaptureSettings s_cachedSettings;
        static double s_nextUpdate;

        static GsplatSceneRelighting()
        {
            SceneView.beforeSceneGui += BeforeSceneGui;
            EditorApplication.update += Update;
            Camera.onPreCull += BeforeCameraRendering;
            RenderPipelineManager.beginCameraRendering += BeginCameraRendering;
            AssemblyReloadEvents.beforeAssemblyReload += CleanupAll;
            EditorApplication.quitting += CleanupAll;
        }

        static void BeforeCameraRendering(Camera camera)
        {
            if (camera && s_states.TryGetValue(camera, out SceneCameraState state))
                camera.cullingMask &= ~state.ManagedProxyLayers;
        }

        static void BeginCameraRendering(ScriptableRenderContext context, Camera camera)
        {
            BeforeCameraRendering(camera);
        }

        static void BeforeSceneGui(SceneView sceneView)
        {
            if (!sceneView || EditorApplication.isCompiling)
                return;

            RefreshCaptureSettingsIfDue();
            SynchronizeSceneView(sceneView, s_cachedSettings, true);
        }

        static void Update()
        {
            if (EditorApplication.isCompiling ||
                EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup < s_nextUpdate)
                return;

            RefreshCaptureSettings();
            var activeCameras = new HashSet<Camera>();
            foreach (SceneView sceneView in SceneView.sceneViews)
            {
                if (!sceneView || !sceneView.camera)
                    continue;

                activeCameras.Add(sceneView.camera);
                SynchronizeSceneView(sceneView, s_cachedSettings, false);
            }

            foreach (Camera camera in s_states.Keys.ToArray())
            {
                if (!camera || !activeCameras.Contains(camera) || !s_cachedSettings.IsValid)
                    Cleanup(camera);
            }
        }

        static void RefreshCaptureSettingsIfDue()
        {
            if (EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup >= s_nextUpdate)
                RefreshCaptureSettings();
        }

        static void RefreshCaptureSettings()
        {
            s_nextUpdate = EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup + 0.2;
            s_cachedSettings = GetCaptureSettings();
        }

        static CaptureSettings GetCaptureSettings()
        {
            var result = new CaptureSettings
            {
                TextureScale = 0.5f,
                Blend = 1f,
                Brightness = 2f,
                Background = 1f
            };
            bool found = false;

            foreach (GsplatRenderer renderer in Object.FindObjectsByType<GsplatRenderer>(
                         FindObjectsInactive.Exclude, FindObjectsSortMode.None))
            {
                if (!renderer.isActiveAndEnabled ||
                    !renderer.TryGetActiveRelighting(out GsplatRelighting source) ||
                    !source.isActiveAndEnabled || source.ProxyLayers.value == 0)
                    continue;

                result.ProxyLayers |= source.ProxyLayers.value;
                result.TextureScale = found
                    ? Mathf.Max(result.TextureScale, source.TextureScale)
                    : source.TextureScale;
                if (!found)
                {
                    result.Blend = source.Blend;
                    result.Brightness = source.Brightness;
                    result.Background = source.Background;
                }

                found = true;
            }

            return result;
        }

        static void SynchronizeSceneView(
            SceneView sceneView,
            CaptureSettings settings,
            bool renderNow)
        {
            Camera camera = sceneView.camera;
            if (!camera)
                return;
            if (!settings.IsValid)
            {
                Cleanup(camera);
                return;
            }

            if (!s_states.TryGetValue(camera, out SceneCameraState state) ||
                !state.Relighting)
            {
                if (state != null)
                    Cleanup(camera);

                GsplatRelighting relighting = camera.GetComponent<GsplatRelighting>();
                bool ownsRelighting = !relighting ||
                                      (relighting.hideFlags & HideFlags.DontSave) != 0;
                if (!relighting)
                    relighting = camera.gameObject.AddComponent<GsplatRelighting>();
                if (!relighting)
                    return;

                if (ownsRelighting)
                    relighting.hideFlags = HideFlags.HideAndDontSave;
                state = new SceneCameraState
                {
                    Camera = camera,
                    Relighting = relighting,
                    OwnsRelighting = ownsRelighting,
                    OriginalRenderOnDemand = relighting.RenderOnDemand
                };
                s_states[camera] = state;
            }

            ApplyProxyLayerExclusion(state, settings.ProxyLayers);
            GsplatRelighting sceneRelighting = state.Relighting;
            sceneRelighting.ProxyLayers = settings.ProxyLayers;
            sceneRelighting.TextureScale = settings.TextureScale;
            sceneRelighting.Blend = settings.Blend;
            sceneRelighting.Brightness = settings.Brightness;
            sceneRelighting.Background = settings.Background;
            sceneRelighting.RenderOnDemand = true;
            if (renderNow)
                sceneRelighting.RenderNow();
            else
                sceneRelighting.Refresh();
        }

        static void ApplyProxyLayerExclusion(SceneCameraState state, int proxyLayers)
        {
            Camera camera = state.Camera;
            if (!camera)
                return;

            int addedLayers = proxyLayers & ~state.ManagedProxyLayers;
            state.OriginalProxyLayerBits =
                (state.OriginalProxyLayerBits & ~addedLayers) |
                (camera.cullingMask & addedLayers);

            int removedLayers = state.ManagedProxyLayers & ~proxyLayers;
            camera.cullingMask =
                (camera.cullingMask & ~removedLayers) |
                (state.OriginalProxyLayerBits & removedLayers);
            state.OriginalProxyLayerBits &= ~removedLayers;
            state.ManagedProxyLayers = proxyLayers;
            camera.cullingMask &= ~proxyLayers;
        }

        static void Cleanup(Camera camera)
        {
            if (!s_states.TryGetValue(camera, out SceneCameraState state))
                return;

            if (state.Camera)
            {
                state.Camera.cullingMask =
                    (state.Camera.cullingMask & ~state.ManagedProxyLayers) |
                    (state.OriginalProxyLayerBits & state.ManagedProxyLayers);
            }

            if (state.OwnsRelighting && state.Relighting)
                Object.DestroyImmediate(state.Relighting);
            else if (state.Relighting)
            {
                state.Relighting.RenderOnDemand = state.OriginalRenderOnDemand;
                state.Relighting.Refresh();
            }
            s_states.Remove(camera);
        }

        static void CleanupAll()
        {
            foreach (Camera camera in s_states.Keys.ToArray())
                Cleanup(camera);
            s_states.Clear();
        }
    }
}
```

### Editor/GsplatSettingsProvider.cs
```
// Copyright (c) 2025 Yize Wu
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Gsplat.Editor
{
    public class GsplatSettingsProvider : SettingsProvider
    {
        SerializedObject m_gsplatSettings;

        public GsplatSettingsProvider(string path, SettingsScope scopes = SettingsScope.Project,
            IEnumerable<string> keywords = null) : base(path, scopes, keywords)
        {
        }

        public override void OnActivate(string searchContext, UnityEngine.UIElements.VisualElement rootElement)
        {
            m_gsplatSettings = new SerializedObject(GsplatSettings.Instance);
        }

        public override void OnGUI(string searchContext)
        {
            m_gsplatSettings.Update();
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(m_gsplatSettings.FindProperty(nameof(GsplatSettings.ComputeShader)));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(m_gsplatSettings.FindProperty(nameof(GsplatSettings.SplatInstanceSize)));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(m_gsplatSettings.FindProperty(nameof(GsplatSettings.UploadBatchSize)));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(m_gsplatSettings.FindProperty(nameof(GsplatSettings.MaxRenderOrder)));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(
                m_gsplatSettings.FindProperty(nameof(GsplatSettings.CameraTranslationRefreshTreshold)));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(
                m_gsplatSettings.FindProperty(nameof(GsplatSettings.CameraRotationRefreshTreshold)));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(m_gsplatSettings.FindProperty(nameof(GsplatSettings.DisplayBoundingBoxes)));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(m_gsplatSettings.FindProperty(nameof(GsplatSettings.ShowImportErrors)));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(m_gsplatSettings.FindProperty(nameof(GsplatSettings.Materials)));

            EditorGUILayout.Space();
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Cross-Renderer Sorting", EditorStyles.boldLabel);
            var enableGlobalSortProp = m_gsplatSettings.FindProperty(nameof(GsplatSettings.EnableGlobalSort));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(enableGlobalSortProp,
                new GUIContent("Enable Cross-Renderer Sorting"));
            if (enableGlobalSortProp.boolValue)
            {
                EditorGUILayout.HelpBox(
                    "Cross-renderer sorting requires the GPU radix path and every active renderer " +
                    "to use Spark compression. " +
                    "If any active renderer uses an uncompressed asset, global sort is disabled " +
                    "for the whole scene and all renderers fall back to per-renderer rendering.",
                    MessageType.Info);
                if (!GsplatSorter.Instance.GpuSortEnabled)
                {
                    EditorGUILayout.HelpBox(
                        "The current graphics API is using the portable CPU sorter. True " +
                        "cross-renderer merging is unavailable on this path. Prefer Vulkan, " +
                        "Direct3D 12, or Metal on supported hardware.",
                        MessageType.Warning);
                }
                EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(m_gsplatSettings.FindProperty(nameof(GsplatSettings.GlobalMaterial)));
            }

            EditorGUILayout.Space();
            GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
            GUILayout.FlexibleSpace();
            if (GUILayout.Button("Reset", GUILayout.Width(60)))
                ResetToDefaults();
            GUILayout.EndHorizontal();
            if (m_gsplatSettings.ApplyModifiedProperties())
                GsplatSorter.Instance.MarkGlobalBuffersDirty();
        }

        void ResetToDefaults()
        {
            Undo.RecordObject(GsplatSettings.Instance, "Reset Gsplat Settings");
            GsplatSettings.Instance.Reset();
            EditorUtility.SetDirty(GsplatSettings.Instance);
            m_gsplatSettings = new SerializedObject(GsplatSettings.Instance);
        }

        [SettingsProvider]
        public static SettingsProvider CreateGsplatSettingsProvider()
        {
            var provider = new GsplatSettingsProvider("Project/Gsplat");
            return provider;
        }
    }
}

### Editor/GsplatUnpackedSogImporter.cs
```
// Copyright (c) 2026 ARLOOPA
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

using System;
using System.IO;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Gsplat.Editor
{
    static class GsplatUnpackedSogImporter
    {
        const string k_MenuRoot = "Assets/UnitySplats/";

        [MenuItem(k_MenuRoot + "Import unpacked SOG (Spark)")]
        static void ImportSpark() => Import(CompressionMode.Spark);

        [MenuItem(k_MenuRoot + "Import unpacked SOG (Uncompressed)")]
        static void ImportUncompressed() => Import(CompressionMode.Uncompressed);

        [MenuItem(k_MenuRoot + "Import unpacked SOG (Spark)", true)]
        [MenuItem(k_MenuRoot + "Import unpacked SOG (Uncompressed)", true)]
        static bool CanImport()
        {
            string path = AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(Selection.activeObject);
            return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(path) &&
                   Path.GetFileName(path).Equals("meta.json", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        }

        static void Import(CompressionMode compression)
        {
            string metadataPath = AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(Selection.activeObject);
            string directory = Path.GetDirectoryName(metadataPath) ?? "Assets";
            string absoluteRoot = Path.GetFullPath(directory).TrimEnd(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar) +
                                  Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;

            byte[] ResolveFile(string relativePath)
            {
                string absolutePath = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(absoluteRoot, relativePath));
                if (!absolutePath.StartsWith(absoluteRoot, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    throw new InvalidDataException(
                        $"SOG file '{relativePath}' resolves outside the metadata directory.");
                return File.ReadAllBytes(absolutePath);
            }

            try
            {
                string metadata = File.ReadAllText(metadataPath);
                ProgressCallback progress = (info, value) =>
                    EditorUtility.DisplayProgressBar("Importing unpacked SOG", info, value);
                GsplatAsset asset = GsplatRuntimeLoader.LoadUnpackedSog(
                    metadata, ResolveFile, compression, SourceCoordinates.RUB, progress);

                string folderName = new DirectoryInfo(directory).Name;
                string outputPath = AssetDatabase.GenerateUniqueAssetPath(
                    Path.Combine(directory, folderName + ".sog.asset").Replace('\\', '/'));
                asset.name = folderName;
                AssetDatabase.CreateAsset(asset, outputPath);
                AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
                Selection.activeObject = asset;
                EditorGUIUtility.PingObject(asset);
                Debug.Log($"Imported unpacked SOG as {outputPath}", asset);
            }
            catch (Exception exception)
            {
                Debug.LogException(exception);
                EditorUtility.DisplayDialog("SOG import failed", exception.Message, "OK");
            }
            finally
            {
                EditorUtility.ClearProgressBar();
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### Editor/GsplatVolumeLightingEditor.cs
```
// Copyright (c) 2026 ARLOOPA
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

using System.Linq;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Gsplat.Editor
{
    [CustomEditor(typeof(GsplatVolumeLighting), true)]
    [CanEditMultipleObjects]
    internal sealed class GsplatVolumeLightingEditor : UnityEditor.Editor
    {
        static readonly string[] s_allRelightingProperties =
        {
            "RelightingMode", "ProxyMesh", "ProxySourceCamera", "ProxyTextureScale",
            "ProxyBlend", "ProxyBrightness", "ProxyBackground", "m_proxyBinding",
            "m_proxySettingsInitialized", "LightMode", "AutomaticLightLimit",
            "SingleLightOverride", "Blend", "AmbientTint", "AmbientStrength",
            "DirectStrength", "DiffuseWrap", "FieldResolution", "FieldDensity",
            "FieldSmoothing", "ShadowStrength", "ShadowSteps", "ShadowBias"
        };

        public override void OnInspectorGUI()
        {
            serializedObject.Update();
            SerializedProperty relightingMode = serializedObject.FindProperty("RelightingMode");
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(relightingMode, new GUIContent("Mode"));

            bool proxyMode = !relightingMode.hasMultipleDifferentValues &&
                             (GsplatRelightingMode)relightingMode.enumValueIndex ==
                             GsplatRelightingMode.ProxyMesh;
            if (proxyMode)
                DrawProxyInspector();
            else
                DrawVolumeInspector();

            DrawPropertiesExcluding(serializedObject,
                new[] { "m_Script" }.Concat(s_allRelightingProperties).ToArray());
            bool changed = serializedObject.ApplyModifiedProperties();

            if (proxyMode)
                DrawProxyStatus(changed);
            else
                DrawVolumeStatus();

            if (changed)
                SceneView.RepaintAll();
        }

        void DrawVolumeInspector()
        {
            EditorGUILayout.Space();
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Volume Relighting", EditorStyles.boldLabel);
            SerializedProperty lightMode = serializedObject.FindProperty("LightMode");
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(lightMode);
            if (!lightMode.hasMultipleDifferentValues &&
                (GsplatVolumeLightMode)lightMode.enumValueIndex ==
                GsplatVolumeLightMode.SingleOverride)
            {
                EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty(
                    "SingleLightOverride"));
            }
            else
            {
                EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty(
                    "AutomaticLightLimit"));
            }
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("Blend"));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("AmbientTint"));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("AmbientStrength"));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("DirectStrength"));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("DiffuseWrap"));
            EditorGUILayout.Space();
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Opacity Self-Occlusion", EditorStyles.boldLabel);
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("FieldResolution"));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("FieldDensity"));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("FieldSmoothing"));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("ShadowStrength"));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("ShadowSteps"));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("ShadowBias"));
        }

        void DrawVolumeStatus()
        {
            EditorGUILayout.Space();
            EditorGUILayout.HelpBox(
                "Volume mode is the default. It shades the Gaussians directly, automatically combines affecting lights, and needs no proxy mesh.",
                MessageType.Info);

            if (targets.Length == 1)
            {
                var lighting = (GsplatVolumeLighting)target;
                int lightCount = lighting.ActiveLightCount;
                if (lightCount == 0)
                    EditorGUILayout.HelpBox(
                        "No active directional, point, or spot light affects this renderer. Only Ambient Tint and Ambient Strength will be used.",
                        MessageType.Warning);
                else if (lighting.LightMode == GsplatVolumeLightMode.Automatic)
                    EditorGUILayout.LabelField(
                        $"Automatic lights: {lightCount}; strongest is {lighting.PrimaryLight.name}",
                        EditorStyles.miniLabel);
                else
                    EditorGUILayout.LabelField(
                        $"Single light override: {lighting.PrimaryLight.name}",
                        EditorStyles.miniLabel);

                string status = lighting.OpacityField
                    ? $"Opacity field ready: {lighting.OpacityField.width}³" +
                      (lighting.OpacityFieldUsers > 1
                          ? $", shared by {lighting.OpacityFieldUsers} renderers"
                          : string.Empty)
                    : lighting.IsOpacityFieldUnavailable
                        ? "Opacity field unavailable; lighting without self-occlusion."
                        : "Opacity field builds lazily when the renderer draws.";
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField(status, EditorStyles.miniLabel);

                if (GUILayout.Button(new GUIContent("Rebuild Gaussian Opacity Field",
                        "Recreates the self-occlusion volume after asset data or field settings change.")))
                {
                    lighting.RebuildOpacityField();
                    SceneView.RepaintAll();
                }
            }
        }

        void DrawProxyInspector()
        {
            EditorGUILayout.Space();
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Proxy Mesh Relighting", EditorStyles.boldLabel);
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("ProxyMesh"));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("ProxySourceCamera"));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("ProxyTextureScale"),
                new GUIContent("Texture Scale"));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("ProxyBlend"),
                new GUIContent("Blend"));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("ProxyBrightness"),
                new GUIContent("Brightness"));
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(serializedObject.FindProperty("ProxyBackground"),
                new GUIContent("Background"));
        }

        void DrawProxyStatus(bool changed)
        {
            EditorGUILayout.Space();
            EditorGUILayout.HelpBox(
                "Proxy Mesh mode uses the existing Lit proxy capture. Assign an aligned mesh child; its backend, material, layer, and camera capture are configured automatically without deleting the Volume settings.",
                MessageType.Info);

            if (targets.Length != 1)
                return;

            var relighting = (GsplatVolumeLighting)target;
            if (!relighting.ProxyMesh)
            {
                EditorGUILayout.HelpBox("Assign an aligned proxy mesh child.", MessageType.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (changed)
                GsplatRelightingSetup.ConfigureUnified(relighting, false);

            bool configured = relighting.ProxyBinding &&
                              relighting.ProxyBinding.RelightingSource;
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField(configured
                    ? "Proxy relighting backend ready."
                    : "Proxy relighting needs configuration.",
                EditorStyles.miniLabel);
            if (GUILayout.Button("Configure Proxy Mesh Relighting"))
            {
                GsplatRelightingSetup.ConfigureUnified(relighting);
                SceneView.RepaintAll();
            }
        }
    }
}

### Runtime/GsplatActiveRange.cs
```
// Copyright (c) 2026 Yize Wu
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

using System;

namespace Gsplat
{
    /// <summary>
    /// A half-open range of source splat IDs: [Offset, Offset + Count).
    /// </summary>
    [Serializable]
    public struct GsplatActiveRange : IEquatable<GsplatActiveRange>
    {
        public uint Offset;
        public uint Count;

        public GsplatActiveRange(uint offset, uint count)
        {
            Offset = offset;
            Count = count;
        }

        public bool Equals(GsplatActiveRange other) => Offset == other.Offset && Count == other.Count;
        public override bool Equals(object obj) => obj is GsplatActiveRange other && Equals(other);
        public override int GetHashCode() => HashCode.Combine(Offset, Count);
        public override string ToString() => $"[{Offset}, {(ulong)Offset + Count})";
    }
}
```

### Runtime/GsplatAsset.cs
```
// Copyright (c) 2025 Yize Wu
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;

namespace Gsplat
{
    public enum CompressionMode
    {
        Uncompressed,
        Spark
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// The coordinate frame the source asset was authored in, using the same naming
    /// convention as the Niantic SPZ library: three letters for X (L/R), Y (U/D), Z (F/B).
    /// The importer converts to Unity (RUF) by applying the appropriate sign flips to
    /// positions, rotation quaternions, and spherical-harmonic coefficients at import time.
    /// </summary>
    public enum SourceCoordinates
    {
        [InspectorName("Auto — GLB: LUF; PLY/SOG/SPZ: RUB")]
        Unspecified = 0,

        [InspectorName("LDB — Left-Down-Back")]
        LDB,

        [InspectorName("RDB — Right-Down-Back")]
        RDB,

        [InspectorName("LUB — Left-Up-Back")]
        LUB,

        [InspectorName("RUB — Right-Up-Back  (3DGS, OpenGL, SPZ)")]
        RUB,

        [InspectorName("LDF — Left-Down-Front")]
        LDF,

        [InspectorName("RDF — Right-Down-Front  (OpenCV, COLMAP)")]
        RDF,

        [InspectorName("LUF — Left-Up-Front  (GLB, glTF)")]
        LUF,

        [InspectorName("RUF — Right-Up-Front  (Unity — no conversion)")]
        RUF,
    }

    public class PlyHeaderInfo
    {
        public uint VertexCount = 0;
        public int PropertyCount = 0;
        public int SHPropertyCount = 0;
        public int PositionOffset = -1;
        public int ColorOffset = -1;
        public int SHOffset = -1;
        public int OpacityOffset = -1;
        public int ScaleOffset = -1;
        public int RotationOffset = -1;

        /// <summary>
        /// Read each line, used for header reading.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="fs"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        static string ReadLine(Stream fs)
        {
            List<byte> byteBuffer = new List<byte>();
            while (true)
            {
                int b = fs.ReadByte();
                if (b == -1 || b == '\n') break;
                byteBuffer.Add((byte)b);
            }

            // If line had CRLF line endings, remove the CR part
            if (byteBuffer.Count > 0 && byteBuffer.Last() == '\r')
            {
                byteBuffer.RemoveAt(byteBuffer.Count - 1);
            }

            return Encoding.UTF8.GetString(byteBuffer.ToArray());
        }

        public PlyHeaderInfo(Stream fs)
        {
            bool inVertexElement = false;
            while (ReadLine(fs) is { } line && line != "end_header")
            {
                var tokens = line.Split(' ');
                if (tokens.Length >= 2 && tokens[0] == "element")
                {
                    inVertexElement = tokens.Length == 3 && tokens[1] == "vertex";
                    if (inVertexElement)
                        VertexCount = uint.Parse(tokens[2]);
                    continue;
                }

                if (!inVertexElement) continue;
                if (tokens.Length != 3 || tokens[0] != "property") continue;
                switch (tokens[2])
                {
                    case "x":
                        PositionOffset = PropertyCount;
                        break;
                    case "f_dc_0":
                        ColorOffset = PropertyCount;
                        break;
                    case "f_rest_0":
                        SHOffset = PropertyCount;
                        break;
                    case "opacity":
                        OpacityOffset = PropertyCount;
                        break;
                    case "scale_0":
                        ScaleOffset = PropertyCount;
                        break;
                    case "rot_0":
                        RotationOffset = PropertyCount;
                        break;
                }

                if (tokens[2].StartsWith("f_rest_"))
                    SHPropertyCount++;
                PropertyCount++;
            }
        }
    }

    public delegate void ProgressCallback(string info, float progress);

    public abstract class GsplatAsset : ScriptableObject
    {
        public uint SplatCount;
        public byte SHBands; // 0, 1, 2, 3, or 4
        [Tooltip("Apply opacity compensation for captures trained with 3DGS antialiasing enabled.")]
        public bool Antialiased;
        public Bounds Bounds;
        public abstract CompressionMode Compression { get; }

        protected int m_kernelInitOrder;
        static readonly protected int k_boundsBuffer = Shader.PropertyToID("_BoundsBuffer");
        static readonly protected int k_cutoutsBuffer = Shader.PropertyToID("_CutoutsBuffer");
        static readonly protected int k_cutoutsCount = Shader.PropertyToID("_CutoutsCount");

        public GsplatMaterial GsplatMaterial => GsplatSettings.Instance.Materials[(int)Compression];
        public Material[] Materials => GsplatMaterial.Materials[SHBands];

        public abstract void Allocate();
        public abstract void LoadFromPly(string plyPath, ProgressCallback progressCallback = null,
            SourceCoordinates sourceCoordinates = SourceCoordinates.RUF);

        /// <summary>Populates this asset from the common decoded representation used by all format readers.</summary>
        public abstract void LoadFromDecoded(GsplatDecodedData data, ProgressCallback progressCallback = null);

        public virtual void LoadFromPlyBytes(byte[] plyBytes, ProgressCallback progressCallback = null,
            SourceCoordinates sourceCoordinates = SourceCoordinates.RUF)
            => throw new NotSupportedException($"{GetType().Name} does not support loading PLY from bytes.");

        public abstract GsplatResource CreateResource();

        public void UploadData(GsplatResource resource)
        {
            if (resource.Uploaded) return;
            _UploadData(resource);
            resource.Uploaded = true;
            resource.UploadedCount = SplatCount;
        }

        public Task UploadDataAsync(GsplatResource resource)
        {
            if (resource == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(resource));
            if (resource.Disposed) throw new ObjectDisposedException(resource.GetType().Name);
            if (resource.Uploaded) return resource.UploadTask;

            var cancellationToken = resource.BeginAsyncUpload();
            resource.Uploaded = true;
            Task task;
            try
            {
                task = _UploadDataAsync(resource, cancellationToken);
            }
            catch (Exception exception)
            {
                // Preserve Task-returning async semantics even if an implementation throws
                // before it reaches its first await.
                task = Task.FromException(exception);
            }

            resource.OwnUploadTask(task);
            return task;
        }

        public GraphicsBuffer UpdateCutoutsBuffer(GraphicsBuffer cutoutsBuffer, GsplatCutout.ShaderData[] cutoutsData)
        {
            var cs = GsplatMaterial.InitOrderShader;
            int numberOfCutouts = cutoutsData.Length;
            int bufferSize = Math.Max(numberOfCutouts, 1);

            if (cutoutsBuffer == null || cutoutsBuffer.count != bufferSize)
            {
                cutoutsBuffer?.Dispose();
                cutoutsBuffer = new GraphicsBuffer(GraphicsBuffer.Target.Structured, bufferSize, GsplatCutout.ShaderDataSize);
            }

            cutoutsBuffer.SetData(cutoutsData);
            cs.SetBuffer(m_kernelInitOrder, k_cutoutsBuffer, cutoutsBuffer);
            cs.SetInt(k_cutoutsCount, numberOfCutouts);
            return cutoutsBuffer;
        }

        public void UpdateBoundsBuffer(GraphicsBuffer BoundsBuffer)
        {
            var cs = GsplatMaterial.InitOrderShader;

            uint max = GsplatUtils.FloatToSortableUint(short.MaxValue);
            uint min = GsplatUtils.FloatToSortableUint(short.MinValue);
            uint[] array = {max, max, max, min, min, min};
            BoundsBuffer.SetData(array);

            cs.SetBuffer(m_kernelInitOrder, k_boundsBuffer, BoundsBuffer);
        }

        protected abstract Task _UploadDataAsync(GsplatResource resource, CancellationToken cancellationToken);

        protected abstract void _UploadData(GsplatResource resource);

        public abstract void SetupMaterialPropertyBlock(MaterialPropertyBlock propertyBlock, GsplatResource resource);

        public abstract void ComputeDepth(CommandBuffer cmd, Matrix4x4 matrixMv,
            ISorterResource sorterResource, GsplatResource resource);

        /// <summary>
        /// Computes depth for the valid OrderBuffer prefix. Custom asset subclasses that have
        /// not adopted active ranges retain the legacy full-resource implementation above.
        /// </summary>
        public virtual void ComputeDepth(CommandBuffer cmd, Matrix4x4 matrixMv, uint activeCount,
            ISorterResource sorterResource, GsplatResource resource) =>
            ComputeDepth(cmd, matrixMv, sorterResource, resource);

        public abstract void InitOrder(ISorterResource sorterResource, GsplatResource resource,
            bool updateBounds);
    }
}
```

### Runtime/GsplatAssetSpark.cs
```
// Copyright (c) 2026 Arthur Aillet, Yize Wu
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Unity.Mathematics;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;
using Gsplat.Formats;

namespace Gsplat
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Implementation taken from SparkJS
    ///
    /// A PackedSplats is a collection of Gaussian splats, packed into a format that
    /// takes exactly 16 bytes per Gsplat to maximize memory and cache efficiency.
    /// The center xyz coordinates are encoded as float16 (3 x 2 bytes), scale xyz
    /// as 3 x uint8 that encode a log scale from e^-12 to e^9, rgba as 4 x uint8,
    /// and quaternion encoded via axis+angle using 2 x uint8 for Octahedral encoding
    /// of the axis direction and a uint8 to encode rotation amount from 0..Pi.
    ///
    /// | Offset (bytes) | Field           | Size (bytes) | Description                                                |
    /// |----------------|-----------------|--------------|------------------------------------------------------------|
    /// | 0              | R               | 1            | Red color channel (uint8 0–255 → 0.0–1.0)                  |
    /// | 1              | G               | 1            | Green color channel (uint8 0–255 → 0.0–1.0)                |
    /// | 2              | B               | 1            | Blue color channel (uint8 0–255 → 0.0–1.0)                 |
    /// | 3              | A               | 1            | Alpha (opacity) channel (uint8 0–255 → 0.0–1.0)            |
    /// | 4–5            | center.x        | 2            | X coordinate of splat center (float16)                     |
    /// | 6–7            | center.y        | 2            | Y coordinate of splat center (float16)                     |
