### Runtime/GsplatGlobalMaterial.cs
```
// Copyright (c) 2026 Keir Rice
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

using UnityEngine;

namespace Gsplat
{
    [CreateAssetMenu(menuName = "Gsplat/Gsplat Global Material")]
    public class GsplatGlobalMaterial : ScriptableObject
    {
        public Material DefaultMaterial;
        public ComputeShader MergeShader;
        public ComputeShader CopyBufferShader;

        Material[] m_materials; // indexed by SH band (0–4)

        /// <summary>
        /// Returns five Material instances — one per SH band level — with the appropriate
        /// SH_BANDS_N keyword pre-enabled. Lazily allocated and cached.
        /// </summary>
        public Material[] Materials
        {
            get
            {
                if (m_materials != null && m_materials[0] != null)
                    return m_materials;

                m_materials = new Material[5];
                for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
                {
                    m_materials[i] = new Material(DefaultMaterial);
                    m_materials[i].DisableKeyword("SH_BANDS_0");
                    m_materials[i].DisableKeyword("SH_BANDS_1");
                    m_materials[i].DisableKeyword("SH_BANDS_2");
                    m_materials[i].DisableKeyword("SH_BANDS_3");
                    m_materials[i].DisableKeyword("SH_BANDS_4");
                    m_materials[i].EnableKeyword($"SH_BANDS_{i}");
                }

                return m_materials;
            }
        }

        public void Reset() => m_materials = null;

        public bool Valid() => MergeShader && CopyBufferShader && DefaultMaterial;
    }
}
```

### Runtime/GsplatGlobalRenderer.cs
```
﻿// Copyright (c) 2026 Keir Rice, Yize Wu
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;

namespace Gsplat
{
    public class GsplatGlobalRenderer
    {
        GsplatGlobalMaterial m_globalMaterial;

        int m_kernelPackOrders = -1;
        int m_kernelMergeTwo = -1;
        int m_kernelMergeTwoWithDepth = -1;
        int m_kernelCopyUint4 = -1;
        int m_kernelCopyUint2 = -1;

        // CPU-side per-renderer metadata (rebuilt when renderers change).
        uint[] m_rendererOffsets; // splat start index in global buffers, per active renderer
        IGsplat[] m_builtRenderers; // renderer identity/order captured when global buffers were last built
        uint[] m_builtSplatCounts; // per-renderer SplatCount captured when the global buffers were last built
        uint m_totalSplatCount;
        uint m_totalRemainingCount; // sum of RemainingCounts; valid entries in GlobalOrderBuffer after merge
        byte m_globalSHBands;
        int m_globalLayer;
        bool m_globalBuffersDirty = true;

        // Global packed data buffers (sub-ranges owned by each renderer).
        GraphicsBuffer m_globalPackedBuffer;
        GraphicsBuffer m_globalSH1Buffer;
        GraphicsBuffer m_globalSH2Buffer;
        GraphicsBuffer m_globalSH3Buffer;
        GraphicsBuffer m_globalSH4Buffer;
        GraphicsBuffer m_globalOrderBuffer;
        GraphicsBuffer m_rendererOffsetsBuffer;
        GraphicsBuffer m_rendererTransformsBuffer;
        GraphicsBuffer m_rendererParamsBuffer;
        GsplatRelighting m_relightingSource;

        // Per-renderer visual settings, looked up by renderer_id during the merged draw.
        // Matches the RendererParams struct in GsplatSparkGlobal.hlsl (48-byte stride).
        [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
        struct RendererParams
        {
            public float Brightness;
            public float ScaleFactor;
            public uint GammaToLinear;
            public uint SHDegree;
            public uint Antialiased;
            public uint RelightEnabled;
            public float RelightBlend;
            public float RelightBrightness;
            public float RelightBackground;
            public uint Padding0;
            public uint Padding1;
            public uint Padding2;
        }

        const int k_rendererParamsStride = 48;

        // Scratch buffers for merge passes.
        // m_packScratch*   : immutable, concatenated per-renderer orders and depths.
        // m_mergeScratchA/B: ping-pong destinations for cascaded intermediate merges.
        // Keeping both merge destinations separate from m_packScratch avoids a GPU
        // read/write overlap once four or more renderers are merged.
        GraphicsBuffer m_packScratchOrders;
        GraphicsBuffer m_packScratchDepths;
        GraphicsBuffer m_mergeScratchAOrders;
        GraphicsBuffer m_mergeScratchADepths;
        GraphicsBuffer m_mergeScratchBOrders;
        GraphicsBuffer m_mergeScratchBDepths;
        Matrix4x4[] m_rendererTransformsCache;
        RendererParams[] m_rendererParamsCache;
        MaterialPropertyBlock m_globalPropertyBlock;

        public bool Valid => m_globalMaterial
                             && m_globalMaterial.Valid()
                             && m_kernelPackOrders >= 0
                             && m_kernelMergeTwo >= 0
                             && m_kernelMergeTwoWithDepth >= 0
                             && m_kernelCopyUint4 >= 0
                             && m_kernelCopyUint2 >= 0;

        const string k_mergePassName = "Gsplat.MergeKWay";

        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Shader property IDs — merge / pack
        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
        static readonly int k_rawOrders = Shader.PropertyToID("_RawOrders");
        static readonly int k_rawDepths = Shader.PropertyToID("_RawDepths");
        static readonly int k_packedOrders = Shader.PropertyToID("_PackedOrders");
        static readonly int k_packedDepths = Shader.PropertyToID("_PackedDepths");
        static readonly int k_rendererIdx = Shader.PropertyToID("_RendererIdx");
        static readonly int k_srcCount = Shader.PropertyToID("_SrcCount");
        static readonly int k_dstOffset = Shader.PropertyToID("_DstOffset");

        static readonly int k_depthsA = Shader.PropertyToID("_DepthsA");
        static readonly int k_ordersA = Shader.PropertyToID("_OrdersA");
        static readonly int k_depthsB = Shader.PropertyToID("_DepthsB");
        static readonly int k_ordersB = Shader.PropertyToID("_OrdersB");
        static readonly int k_offsetA = Shader.PropertyToID("_OffsetA");
        static readonly int k_offsetB = Shader.PropertyToID("_OffsetB");
        static readonly int k_countA = Shader.PropertyToID("_CountA");
        static readonly int k_countB = Shader.PropertyToID("_CountB");
        static readonly int k_outputOffset = Shader.PropertyToID("_OutputOffset");
        static readonly int k_outputOrders = Shader.PropertyToID("_OutputOrders");
        static readonly int k_outputDepths = Shader.PropertyToID("_OutputDepths");

        // Copy kernel IDs
        static readonly int k_srcUint4 = Shader.PropertyToID("_SrcUint4");
        static readonly int k_dstUint4 = Shader.PropertyToID("_DstUint4");
        static readonly int k_srcUint2 = Shader.PropertyToID("_SrcUint2");
        static readonly int k_dstUint2 = Shader.PropertyToID("_DstUint2");
        static readonly int k_srcElementCount = Shader.PropertyToID("_SrcElementCount");
        static readonly int k_dstElementOffset = Shader.PropertyToID("_DstElementOffset");

        // Global draw material properties
        static readonly int k_globalOrderBuffer = Shader.PropertyToID("_GlobalOrderBuffer");
        static readonly int k_globalPackedBuffer = Shader.PropertyToID("_GlobalPackedBuffer");
        static readonly int k_globalSH1Buffer = Shader.PropertyToID("_GlobalSH1Buffer");
        static readonly int k_globalSH2Buffer = Shader.PropertyToID("_GlobalSH2Buffer");
        static readonly int k_globalSH3Buffer = Shader.PropertyToID("_GlobalSH3Buffer");
        static readonly int k_globalSH4Buffer = Shader.PropertyToID("_GlobalSH4Buffer");
        static readonly int k_rendererOffsetsProp = Shader.PropertyToID("_RendererOffsets");
        static readonly int k_rendererTransformsProp = Shader.PropertyToID("_RendererTransforms");
        static readonly int k_rendererParamsProp = Shader.PropertyToID("_RendererParams");
        static readonly int k_totalSplatCount = Shader.PropertyToID("_TotalSplatCount");
        static readonly int k_splatInstanceSize = Shader.PropertyToID("_SplatInstanceSize");
        static readonly int k_relightMap = Shader.PropertyToID("_GsplatRelightMap");
        static readonly int k_relightScreen = Shader.PropertyToID("_GsplatRelightScreen");

        public void InitGlobal(GsplatGlobalMaterial globalMaterial)
        {
            m_globalMaterial = globalMaterial;
            m_kernelPackOrders = -1;
            m_kernelMergeTwo = -1;
            m_kernelMergeTwoWithDepth = -1;
            m_kernelCopyUint4 = -1;
            m_kernelCopyUint2 = -1;

            if (!SystemInfo.supportsComputeShaders)
            {
                m_globalMaterial = null;
                DisposeGlobalBuffers();
                m_globalBuffersDirty = true;
                return;
            }

            var mergeShader = globalMaterial ? globalMaterial.MergeShader : null;
            var copyShader = globalMaterial ? globalMaterial.CopyBufferShader : null;

            if (mergeShader)
            {
                if (!mergeShader.HasKernel("PackRendererOrders"))
                    Debug.LogError(
                        $"[GsplatSorter] MergeShader '{mergeShader.name}' is missing kernel 'PackRendererOrders' — assign GsplatMergeOrderBuffers.compute.");
                else
                {
                    m_kernelPackOrders = mergeShader.FindKernel("PackRendererOrders");
                    m_kernelMergeTwo = mergeShader.FindKernel("MergeTwo");
                    m_kernelMergeTwoWithDepth = mergeShader.FindKernel("MergeTwoWithDepth");
                }
            }

            if (copyShader)
            {
                if (!copyShader.HasKernel("CopyUint4"))
                    Debug.LogError(
                        $"[GsplatSorter] CopyBufferShader '{copyShader.name}' is missing kernel 'CopyUint4' — assign GsplatCopyBuffer.compute.");
                else
                {
                    m_kernelCopyUint4 = copyShader.FindKernel("CopyUint4");
                    m_kernelCopyUint2 = copyShader.FindKernel("CopyUint2");
                }
            }

            m_globalBuffersDirty = true;
        }

        public void Update(List<IGsplat> activeGsplats)
        {
            m_globalLayer = activeGsplats.Count > 0
                ? (activeGsplats[0] as Component)?.gameObject.layer ?? 0
                : 0;
            EnsureGlobalBuffers(activeGsplats);
            if (m_totalSplatCount == 0) return;

            // The camera's sort pass executes before transparent drawing and fills the new
            // order buffer. Seed the draw count from current renderer state so the first
            // frame after a rebuild is rendered instead of waiting for a previous merge.
            m_totalRemainingCount = 0;
            foreach (var gsplat in activeGsplats)
                m_totalRemainingCount = checked(m_totalRemainingCount + gsplat.RemainingCount);

            UpdateRendererTransforms(activeGsplats);
            UpdateRendererParams(activeGsplats);
            Render();
        }

        public void DispatchMerge(CommandBuffer cmd, List<IGsplat> activeGsplats)
        {
            if (m_totalSplatCount == 0) return;
            cmd.BeginSample(k_mergePassName);
            DispatchMergeInternal(cmd, activeGsplats);
            cmd.EndSample(k_mergePassName);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Call when a renderer's asset changes so the global packed buffer is rebuilt next frame.
        /// </summary>
        public void MarkGlobalBuffersDirty() => m_globalBuffersDirty = true;

        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Global buffer management
        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
        void EnsureGlobalBuffers(List<IGsplat> activeGsplats)
        {
            // A renderer streaming in its splats via async upload (AsyncUpload +
            // RenderBeforeUploadComplete) grows its SplatCount frame to frame. Rebuild when an
            // active renderer's count no longer matches what we last built with, otherwise the
            // global sub-ranges keep only the splats present at the first build and the rest are
            // never copied in (leaving the merge to read stale/under-sized global buffers).
            if (!m_globalBuffersDirty && ActiveSplatCountsChanged(activeGsplats))
                m_globalBuffersDirty = true;

            if (!m_globalBuffersDirty) return;
            m_globalBuffersDirty = false;

            DisposeGlobalBuffers();

            m_totalSplatCount = 0;
            m_rendererOffsets = new uint[activeGsplats.Count];
            m_builtRenderers = new IGsplat[activeGsplats.Count];
            m_builtSplatCounts = new uint[activeGsplats.Count];
            // Size SH buffers to the highest band count in the set; lower-band renderers
            // occupy the extra slots but never read them, since each renderer's SHDegree is
            // clamped to its own asset bands in UpdateRendererParams.
            m_globalSHBands = 0;

            foreach (var gs in activeGsplats.Where(gs => gs.SHBands > m_globalSHBands))
                m_globalSHBands = gs.SHBands;

            for (int k = 0; k < activeGsplats.Count; k++)
            {
                var count = activeGsplats[k].SplatCount;
                m_rendererOffsets[k] = m_totalSplatCount;
                m_builtRenderers[k] = activeGsplats[k];
                m_builtSplatCounts[k] = count;
                m_totalSplatCount += count;
            }

            if (m_totalSplatCount == 0) return;

            // CanRenderGlobally() in Gather already guarded count<=255 and Spark-only;
            // GlobalRenderEnabled would be false and we wouldn't be here otherwise.

            var st = GraphicsBuffer.Target.Structured;
            m_globalPackedBuffer = new GraphicsBuffer(st, (int)m_totalSplatCount, sizeof(uint) * 4)
                { name = "Gsplat.GlobalPacked" };
            m_globalOrderBuffer = new GraphicsBuffer(st, (int)m_totalSplatCount, sizeof(uint))
                { name = "Gsplat.GlobalOrder" };
            m_rendererOffsetsBuffer = new GraphicsBuffer(st, activeGsplats.Count, sizeof(uint))
                { name = "Gsplat.RendererOffsets" };
            m_rendererTransformsBuffer = new GraphicsBuffer(st, activeGsplats.Count, sizeof(float) * 16)
                { name = "Gsplat.RendererTransforms" };
            Debug.Assert(Marshal.SizeOf<RendererParams>() == k_rendererParamsStride,
                $"RendererParams C# stride must match HLSL ({k_rendererParamsStride} bytes).");
            m_rendererParamsBuffer = new GraphicsBuffer(st, activeGsplats.Count, k_rendererParamsStride)
                { name = "Gsplat.RendererParams" };

            if (m_globalSHBands >= 1)
                m_globalSH1Buffer = new GraphicsBuffer(st, (int)m_totalSplatCount, sizeof(uint) * 2)
                    { name = "Gsplat.GlobalSH1" };
            if (m_globalSHBands >= 2)
                m_globalSH2Buffer = new GraphicsBuffer(st, (int)m_totalSplatCount, sizeof(uint) * 4)
                    { name = "Gsplat.GlobalSH2" };
            if (m_globalSHBands >= 3)
                m_globalSH3Buffer = new GraphicsBuffer(st, (int)m_totalSplatCount, sizeof(uint) * 4)
                    { name = "Gsplat.GlobalSH3" };
            if (m_globalSHBands >= 4)
                m_globalSH4Buffer = new GraphicsBuffer(st, (int)m_totalSplatCount, sizeof(uint) * 4)
                    { name = "Gsplat.GlobalSH4" };

            m_rendererOffsetsBuffer.SetData(m_rendererOffsets);

            // Copy per-renderer packed splat data into global buffer sub-ranges.
            for (int k = 0; k < activeGsplats.Count; k++)
            {
                var gs = activeGsplats[k];
                var count = gs.SplatCount;
                var off = m_rendererOffsets[k];
                if (count == 0) continue;

                var res = (GsplatResourceSpark)gs.GsplatResource;

                CopyUint4(res.PackedSplatsBuffer, m_globalPackedBuffer, count, off);
                if (m_globalSHBands >= 1 && res.PackedSH1Buffer != null)
                    CopyUint2(res.PackedSH1Buffer, m_globalSH1Buffer, count, off);
                if (m_globalSHBands >= 2 && res.PackedSH2Buffer != null)
                    CopyUint4(res.PackedSH2Buffer, m_globalSH2Buffer, count, off);
                if (m_globalSHBands >= 3 && res.PackedSH3Buffer != null)
                    CopyUint4(res.PackedSH3Buffer, m_globalSH3Buffer, count, off);
                if (m_globalSHBands >= 4 && res.PackedSH4Buffer != null)
                    CopyUint4(res.PackedSH4Buffer, m_globalSH4Buffer, count, off);
            }

            // Allocate merge scratch buffers (always fresh after a dirty rebuild).
            m_packScratchOrders = new GraphicsBuffer(st, (int)m_totalSplatCount, sizeof(uint))
                { name = "Gsplat.PackScratchOrders" };
            m_packScratchDepths = new GraphicsBuffer(st, (int)m_totalSplatCount, sizeof(float))
                { name = "Gsplat.PackScratchDepths" };
            if (activeGsplats.Count > 2)
            {
                m_mergeScratchAOrders = new GraphicsBuffer(st, (int)m_totalSplatCount, sizeof(uint))
                    { name = "Gsplat.MergeScratchAOrders" };
                m_mergeScratchADepths = new GraphicsBuffer(st, (int)m_totalSplatCount, sizeof(float))
                    { name = "Gsplat.MergeScratchADepths" };
            }
            if (activeGsplats.Count > 3)
            {
                m_mergeScratchBOrders = new GraphicsBuffer(st, (int)m_totalSplatCount, sizeof(uint))
                    { name = "Gsplat.MergeScratchBOrders" };
                m_mergeScratchBDepths = new GraphicsBuffer(st, (int)m_totalSplatCount, sizeof(float))
                    { name = "Gsplat.MergeScratchBDepths" };
            }
        }

        // True when active renderer identity/order or SplatCount no longer matches the global build.
        // Identity matters because a resident renderer can enter or leave the active set without a
        // register/unregister event when its explicit range crosses zero.
        bool ActiveSplatCountsChanged(List<IGsplat> activeGsplats)
        {
            if (m_builtRenderers == null || m_builtSplatCounts == null ||
                m_builtRenderers.Length != activeGsplats.Count || m_builtSplatCounts.Length != activeGsplats.Count)
                return true;
            return activeGsplats.Where((t, k) =>
                !ReferenceEquals(m_builtRenderers[k], t) || m_builtSplatCounts[k] != t.SplatCount).Any();
        }

        void UpdateRendererTransforms(List<IGsplat> activeGsplats)
        {
            if (m_rendererTransformsBuffer == null) return;
            var count = activeGsplats.Count;
            if (m_rendererTransformsCache == null || m_rendererTransformsCache.Length != count)
                m_rendererTransformsCache = new Matrix4x4[count];
            for (int k = 0; k < count; k++)
                m_rendererTransformsCache[k] = activeGsplats[k].transform.localToWorldMatrix;
            m_rendererTransformsBuffer.SetData(m_rendererTransformsCache);
        }

        void UpdateRendererParams(List<IGsplat> activeGsplats)
        {
            if (m_rendererParamsBuffer == null) return;
            var count = activeGsplats.Count;
            if (m_rendererParamsCache == null || m_rendererParamsCache.Length != count)
                m_rendererParamsCache = new RendererParams[count];
            m_relightingSource = null;
            for (int k = 0; k < count; k++)
            {
                var gs = activeGsplats[k] as GsplatRenderer;
                if (gs != null)
                {
                    bool relightingEnabled = gs.TryGetActiveRelighting(
                        out var relighting,
                        out float relightBlend,
                        out float relightBrightness,
                        out float relightBackground);
                    if (relightingEnabled && !m_relightingSource)
                        m_relightingSource = relighting;
                    m_rendererParamsCache[k] = new RendererParams
                    {
                        Brightness = gs.Brightness,
                        ScaleFactor = 1.0f - gs.SplatDownscaleFactor,
                        GammaToLinear = gs.GammaToLinear ? 1u : 0u,
                        // Clamp to the asset's own bands so EvalSH never reads slots that
                        // weren't copied for this renderer (the global buffers are sized to
                        // the max bands across the set).
                        SHDegree = (uint)Mathf.Min(gs.SHDegree, activeGsplats[k].SHBands),
                        Antialiased = gs.GsplatAsset && gs.GsplatAsset.Antialiased ? 1u : 0u,
                        RelightEnabled = relightingEnabled ? 1u : 0u,
                        RelightBlend = relightingEnabled ? relightBlend : 0f,
                        RelightBrightness = relightingEnabled ? relightBrightness : 0f,
                        RelightBackground = relightingEnabled ? relightBackground : 1f,
                    };
                }
                else
                {
                    m_rendererParamsCache[k] = new RendererParams
                    {
                        Brightness = 1.0f,
                        ScaleFactor = 1.0f,
                        GammaToLinear = 0u,
                        SHDegree = activeGsplats[k].SHBands,
                        Antialiased = 0u,
                        RelightBackground = 1f,
                    };
                }
            }

            m_rendererParamsBuffer.SetData(m_rendererParamsCache);
        }

        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Copy helpers
        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
        void CopyUint4(GraphicsBuffer src, GraphicsBuffer dst, uint count, uint dstOff)
        {
            var cs = m_globalMaterial.CopyBufferShader;
            cs.SetInt(k_srcElementCount, (int)count);
            cs.SetInt(k_dstElementOffset, (int)dstOff);
            cs.SetBuffer(m_kernelCopyUint4, k_srcUint4, src);
            cs.SetBuffer(m_kernelCopyUint4, k_dstUint4, dst);
            cs.Dispatch(m_kernelCopyUint4, (int)GsplatUtils.DivRoundUp(count, 256), 1, 1);
        }

        void CopyUint2(GraphicsBuffer src, GraphicsBuffer dst, uint count, uint dstOff)
        {
            var cs = m_globalMaterial.CopyBufferShader;
            cs.SetInt(k_srcElementCount, (int)count);
            cs.SetInt(k_dstElementOffset, (int)dstOff);
            cs.SetBuffer(m_kernelCopyUint2, k_srcUint2, src);
            cs.SetBuffer(m_kernelCopyUint2, k_dstUint2, dst);
            cs.Dispatch(m_kernelCopyUint2, (int)GsplatUtils.DivRoundUp(count, 256), 1, 1);
        }

        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Merge dispatch
        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
        void DispatchMergeInternal(CommandBuffer cmd, List<IGsplat> activeGsplats)
        {
            int K = activeGsplats.Count;
            if (K < 2 || m_globalOrderBuffer == null) return;

            // Step 1: pack each renderer's sorted orders + depths into m_packScratch.
            for (int k = 0; k < K; k++)
            {
                var gs = activeGsplats[k];
                if (gs.SorterResource is not { } res) continue;
                uint cnt = gs.RemainingCount;
                uint off = m_rendererOffsets[k];
                if (cnt == 0) continue;

                cmd.SetComputeIntParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, k_rendererIdx, k);
                cmd.SetComputeIntParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, k_srcCount, (int)cnt);
                cmd.SetComputeIntParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, k_dstOffset, (int)off);
                cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, m_kernelPackOrders, k_rawOrders,
                    res.OrderBuffer);
                cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, m_kernelPackOrders, k_rawDepths, res.InputKeys);
                cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, m_kernelPackOrders, k_packedOrders,
                    m_packScratchOrders);
                cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, m_kernelPackOrders, k_packedDepths,
                    m_packScratchDepths);
                cmd.DispatchCompute(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, m_kernelPackOrders,
                    (int)GsplatUtils.DivRoundUp(cnt, 256), 1, 1);
            }

            // Step 2: cascaded 2-way merges. The packed buffer remains immutable: each
            // renderer's source range always comes from it, while intermediate merged
            // results ping-pong between two distinct scratch pairs.

            uint mergedCount = activeGsplats[0].RemainingCount; // size of merged result so far
            uint mergedOffset = 0; // always at start of its scratch buffer

            GraphicsBuffer currentOrders = m_packScratchOrders;
            GraphicsBuffer currentDepths = m_packScratchDepths;
            bool writeScratchA = true;

            for (int k = 1; k < K; k++)
            {
                var gs = activeGsplats[k];
                uint cntK = gs.RemainingCount;
                uint offK = m_rendererOffsets[k];
                bool isFinal = (k == K - 1);
                uint total = mergedCount + cntK;
                if (total == 0)
                    continue;

                // Source B: renderer k's range in packScratch.
                var srcBOrders = m_packScratchOrders;
                var srcBDepths = m_packScratchDepths;

                // Destination: final pass → globalOrderBuffer; otherwise the other scratch.
                GraphicsBuffer dstOrders, dstDepths;
                if (isFinal)
                {
                    dstOrders = m_globalOrderBuffer;
                    dstDepths = null;
                }
                else
                {
                    dstOrders = writeScratchA ? m_mergeScratchAOrders : m_mergeScratchBOrders;
                    dstDepths = writeScratchA ? m_mergeScratchADepths : m_mergeScratchBDepths;
                }

                int kernel = isFinal ? m_kernelMergeTwo : m_kernelMergeTwoWithDepth;

                cmd.SetComputeIntParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, k_offsetA, (int)mergedOffset);
                cmd.SetComputeIntParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, k_offsetB, (int)offK);
                cmd.SetComputeIntParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, k_countA, (int)mergedCount);
                cmd.SetComputeIntParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, k_countB, (int)cntK);
                cmd.SetComputeIntParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, k_outputOffset, 0);
                cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, kernel, k_depthsA, currentDepths);
                cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, kernel, k_ordersA, currentOrders);
                cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, kernel, k_depthsB, srcBDepths);
                cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, kernel, k_ordersB, srcBOrders);
                cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, kernel, k_outputOrders, dstOrders);
                if (!isFinal)
                    cmd.SetComputeBufferParam(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, kernel, k_outputDepths, dstDepths);

                cmd.DispatchCompute(m_globalMaterial.MergeShader, kernel, (int)GsplatUtils.DivRoundUp(total, 256), 1,
                    1);

                mergedCount = total;
                mergedOffset = 0; // output always starts at 0 in the destination buffer
                if (!isFinal)
                {
                    currentOrders = dstOrders;
                    currentDepths = dstDepths;
                    writeScratchA = !writeScratchA;
                }
            }

            m_totalRemainingCount = mergedCount;
        }

        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Global draw call
        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
        void Render()
        {
            // m_globalBuffersDirty: the active renderer set changed and the next DispatchSort
            // hasn't validated/rebuilt the buffers yet. Drawing now would bind stale buffers
            // (under URP, DrawAllIfEnabled fires before DispatchSort each frame).
            if (m_globalBuffersDirty || m_globalOrderBuffer == null || m_totalRemainingCount == 0) return;

            // Bind buffers via a MaterialPropertyBlock rather than on the material itself, so
            // each queued draw captures its own bindings and multiple cameras (Game + SceneView,
            // or any multi-camera setup) don't overwrite one another's state before the GPU
            // executes them.
            m_globalPropertyBlock ??= new MaterialPropertyBlock();
            m_globalPropertyBlock.Clear();
            m_globalPropertyBlock.SetBuffer(k_globalOrderBuffer, m_globalOrderBuffer);
            m_globalPropertyBlock.SetBuffer(k_globalPackedBuffer, m_globalPackedBuffer);
            m_globalPropertyBlock.SetBuffer(k_rendererOffsetsProp, m_rendererOffsetsBuffer);
            m_globalPropertyBlock.SetBuffer(k_rendererTransformsProp, m_rendererTransformsBuffer);
            m_globalPropertyBlock.SetBuffer(k_rendererParamsProp, m_rendererParamsBuffer);
            m_globalPropertyBlock.SetInteger(k_totalSplatCount, (int)m_totalRemainingCount);
            m_globalPropertyBlock.SetInteger(k_splatInstanceSize, (int)GsplatSettings.Instance.SplatInstanceSize);
            m_globalPropertyBlock.SetTexture(k_relightMap,
                m_relightingSource && m_relightingSource.IsReady
                    ? m_relightingSource.RelightingTexture
                    : Texture2D.blackTexture);
            m_globalPropertyBlock.SetVector(k_relightScreen,
                m_relightingSource && m_relightingSource.IsReady
                    ? m_relightingSource.SourceScreen
                    : Vector4.zero);

            if (m_globalSHBands >= 1)
                m_globalPropertyBlock.SetBuffer(k_globalSH1Buffer, m_globalSH1Buffer);
            if (m_globalSHBands >= 2)
                m_globalPropertyBlock.SetBuffer(k_globalSH2Buffer, m_globalSH2Buffer);
            if (m_globalSHBands >= 3)
                m_globalPropertyBlock.SetBuffer(k_globalSH3Buffer, m_globalSH3Buffer);
            if (m_globalSHBands >= 4)
                m_globalPropertyBlock.SetBuffer(k_globalSH4Buffer, m_globalSH4Buffer);

            var rp = new RenderParams(m_globalMaterial.Materials[m_globalSHBands])
            {
                worldBounds = new Bounds(Vector3.zero, Vector3.one * 1e6f),
                matProps = m_globalPropertyBlock,
                layer = m_globalLayer
            };

            int instances = Mathf.CeilToInt(m_totalRemainingCount / (float)GsplatSettings.Instance.SplatInstanceSize);
            Graphics.RenderMeshPrimitives(rp, GsplatSettings.Instance.Mesh, 0, instances);
        }


        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Cleanup
        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
        public void DisposeGlobalBuffers()
        {
            // A rebuilt order buffer has no valid merged entries until DispatchMerge writes it.
            // Clear both counts before disposing so Update cannot draw a fresh/uninitialized
            // buffer using the previous build's m_totalRemainingCount.
            m_totalSplatCount = 0;
            m_totalRemainingCount = 0;

            m_globalPackedBuffer?.Dispose();
            m_globalPackedBuffer = null;
            m_globalSH1Buffer?.Dispose();
            m_globalSH1Buffer = null;
            m_globalSH2Buffer?.Dispose();
            m_globalSH2Buffer = null;
            m_globalSH3Buffer?.Dispose();
            m_globalSH3Buffer = null;
            m_globalSH4Buffer?.Dispose();
            m_globalSH4Buffer = null;
            m_globalOrderBuffer?.Dispose();
            m_globalOrderBuffer = null;
            m_rendererOffsetsBuffer?.Dispose();
            m_rendererOffsetsBuffer = null;
            m_rendererTransformsBuffer?.Dispose();
            m_rendererTransformsBuffer = null;
            m_rendererParamsBuffer?.Dispose();
            m_rendererParamsBuffer = null;
            m_relightingSource = null;
            // Scratch buffers are rebuilt every time global buffers are rebuilt,
            // so dispose them here too to avoid leaking when renderer count shrinks.
            m_packScratchOrders?.Dispose();
            m_packScratchOrders = null;
            m_packScratchDepths?.Dispose();
            m_packScratchDepths = null;
            m_mergeScratchAOrders?.Dispose();
            m_mergeScratchAOrders = null;
            m_mergeScratchADepths?.Dispose();
            m_mergeScratchADepths = null;
            m_mergeScratchBOrders?.Dispose();
            m_mergeScratchBOrders = null;
            m_mergeScratchBDepths?.Dispose();
            m_mergeScratchBDepths = null;
            // Drop the build snapshot so the next EnsureGlobalBuffers re-captures the active set.
            m_builtRenderers = null;
            m_builtSplatCounts = null;
        }
    }
}
```

### Runtime/GsplatMaterial.cs
```
// Copyright (c) 2026 Yize Wu
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

using UnityEngine;

namespace Gsplat
{
    [CreateAssetMenu(menuName = "Gsplat/Gsplat Material")]
    public class GsplatMaterial : ScriptableObject
    {
        public Material DefaultMaterial;
        private Material[][] m_material;

        public void Reset()
        {
            m_material = null;
        }

        public Material[][] Materials // materials generated with SH bands from 0 to 4 and custom renderOrders
        {
            get
            {
                if (m_material != null && m_material[0][0] != null)
                    return m_material;

                m_material = new Material[5][];
                for (var i = 0; i < 5; ++i)
                {
                    m_material[i] = new Material[GsplatSettings.Instance.MaxRenderOrder];
                    for (var j = 0; j < GsplatSettings.Instance.MaxRenderOrder; ++j)
                    {
                        m_material[i][j] = new Material(DefaultMaterial);
                        m_material[i][j].DisableKeyword($"SH_BANDS_0");
                        m_material[i][j].DisableKeyword($"SH_BANDS_1");
                        m_material[i][j].DisableKeyword($"SH_BANDS_2");
                        m_material[i][j].DisableKeyword($"SH_BANDS_3");
                        m_material[i][j].DisableKeyword($"SH_BANDS_4");
                        m_material[i][j].EnableKeyword($"SH_BANDS_{i}");
                        if (GsplatUtils.UseVertexDataTextures)
                        {
                            m_material[i][j].EnableKeyword(GsplatUtils.k_VertexTextureKeyword);
                            m_material[i][j].DisableKeyword(GsplatUtils.k_VertexBufferKeyword);
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            m_material[i][j].DisableKeyword(GsplatUtils.k_VertexTextureKeyword);
                            m_material[i][j].EnableKeyword(GsplatUtils.k_VertexBufferKeyword);
                        }
                        m_material[i][j].renderQueue = 3000 + j;
                    }
                }
                return m_material;
            }
        }

        public ComputeShader CalcDepthShader;
        public ComputeShader InitOrderShader;
    }
}

### Runtime/GsplatPlayerLoopHook.cs
```
// Copyright (c) 2026 Yize Wu
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
#if UNITY_EDITOR
using UnityEditor;
#endif
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.LowLevel;
using UnityEngine.PlayerLoop;


namespace Gsplat
{
#if UNITY_EDITOR
    [InitializeOnLoad]
#endif
    public static class GsplatPlayerLoopHook
    {
#if UNITY_EDITOR
        static GsplatPlayerLoopHook()
        {
            Install();
            EditorApplication.playModeStateChanged += _ => { Install(); };
        }
#endif

        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.SubsystemRegistration)]
        static void RuntimeInit()
        {
            // Entering play mode can reset Unity's player loop without resetting managed
            // statics when domain reload is disabled.
            Install();
        }

        static void Install()
        {
            var loop = PlayerLoop.GetCurrentPlayerLoop();
            if (Contains(ref loop, typeof(GsplatPlayerLoopHook)))
                return;

            if (!InsertBefore<PostLateUpdate>(ref loop, typeof(GsplatPlayerLoopHook), Update))
            {
                Debug.LogError("[Gsplat] Could not install the Gaussian-splat player-loop hook.");
                return;
            }
            PlayerLoop.SetPlayerLoop(loop);
        }

        static bool Contains(ref PlayerLoopSystem root, System.Type type)
        {
            if (root.type == type)
                return true;
            if (root.subSystemList == null)
                return false;
            for (var i = 0; i < root.subSystemList.Length; ++i)
                if (Contains(ref root.subSystemList[i], type))
                    return true;
            return false;
        }

        static bool InsertBefore<T>(ref PlayerLoopSystem root, System.Type type, PlayerLoopSystem.UpdateFunction fn)
        {
            if (root.subSystemList == null)
                return false;

            for (var i = 0; i < root.subSystemList.Length; i++)
            {
                ref var sys = ref root.subSystemList[i];
                if (sys.type == typeof(T))
                {
                    var list = sys.subSystemList?.ToList() ?? new List<PlayerLoopSystem>();
                    list.Insert(0, new PlayerLoopSystem { type = type, updateDelegate = fn });
                    sys.subSystemList = list.ToArray();
                    return true;
                }

                if (InsertBefore<T>(ref sys, type, fn))
                    return true;
            }

            return false;
        }

        static void Update()
        {
            // Camera controllers commonly finalize their transform in LateUpdate. Synchronize
            // the proxy now, then let Unity render its target-texture camera through the normal
            // camera loop before the Game camera. Manual SRP render requests can produce a
            // different result from the normal Play-mode camera path.
            GsplatRelighting.SynchronizeGameCameras();
            GsplatSorter.Instance.Update();
        }
    }
}
```

### Runtime/GsplatProxyAnimator.cs
```
// Copyright (c) 2026 ARLOOPA
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Experimental.Rendering;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;
using Debug = UnityEngine.Debug;

namespace Gsplat
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Deforms a Gaussian splat with the bones and skin weights of an aligned proxy hierarchy.
    /// Bindings are generated from the closest bind-pose triangle across all child proxy meshes,
    /// with barycentrically interpolated skin weights. They are kept only at runtime; the
    /// imported Gaussian asset is never modified.
    /// </summary>
    [ExecuteAlways]
    [DefaultExecutionOrder(10000)]
    [DisallowMultipleComponent]
    [RequireComponent(typeof(GsplatRenderer))]
    [AddComponentMenu("Gaussian Splat/Proxy Mesh Animator")]
    public sealed class GsplatProxyAnimator : GsplatSkinningSource
    {
        [Tooltip("Optional primary skinned renderer. Proxy Root is preferred when the proxy contains multiple child meshes.")]
        public SkinnedMeshRenderer ProxyRenderer;

        [Tooltip("Root containing every proxy mesh. Leave empty to use the first child Animator hierarchy, or the assigned Proxy Renderer hierarchy.")]
        public Transform ProxyRoot;

        [Tooltip("Updates the Gaussian while previewing animation outside Play mode.")]
        public bool AnimateInEditMode;

        [Tooltip("Hides every Renderer under Proxy Root while retaining the hierarchy as the Gaussian deformation driver.")]
        public bool HideProxyRenderer = true;

        [Tooltip("Extra local-space bounds around the union of the original splat and animated proxy bounds.")]
        [Min(0f)] public float BoundsPadding = 0.1f;

        [Tooltip("In the Editor, compares this component's skinning against Unity's baked proxy mesh once shortly after animation starts.")]
        public bool ValidateSkinningAgainstProxy = true;

        [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
        internal struct SkinWeightData
        {
            public uint BoneIndex0;
            public uint BoneIndex1;
            public uint BoneIndex2;
            public uint BoneIndex3;
            public float Weight0;
            public float Weight1;
            public float Weight2;
            public float Weight3;

            public SkinWeightData(BoneWeight source, int boneCount) : this(source, boneCount, 0)
            {
            }

            public SkinWeightData(BoneWeight source, int boneCount, int boneOffset)
            {
                bool valid0 = IsValidBoneIndex(source.boneIndex0, boneCount);
                bool valid1 = IsValidBoneIndex(source.boneIndex1, boneCount);
                bool valid2 = IsValidBoneIndex(source.boneIndex2, boneCount);
                bool valid3 = IsValidBoneIndex(source.boneIndex3, boneCount);
                BoneIndex0 = valid0 ? checked((uint)(source.boneIndex0 + boneOffset)) : 0u;
                BoneIndex1 = valid1 ? checked((uint)(source.boneIndex1 + boneOffset)) : 0u;
                BoneIndex2 = valid2 ? checked((uint)(source.boneIndex2 + boneOffset)) : 0u;
                BoneIndex3 = valid3 ? checked((uint)(source.boneIndex3 + boneOffset)) : 0u;
                Weight0 = valid0 ? Mathf.Max(0f, source.weight0) : 0f;
                Weight1 = valid1 ? Mathf.Max(0f, source.weight1) : 0f;
                Weight2 = valid2 ? Mathf.Max(0f, source.weight2) : 0f;
                Weight3 = valid3 ? Mathf.Max(0f, source.weight3) : 0f;

                float total = Weight0 + Weight1 + Weight2 + Weight3;
                if (total <= 1e-8f)
                {
                    BoneIndex0 = BoneIndex1 = BoneIndex2 = BoneIndex3 = 0;
                    Weight0 = 1f;
                    Weight1 = Weight2 = Weight3 = 0f;
                    return;
                }

                float inverseTotal = 1f / total;
                Weight0 *= inverseTotal;
                Weight1 *= inverseTotal;
                Weight2 *= inverseTotal;
                Weight3 *= inverseTotal;
            }

            public static SkinWeightData Rigid(int boneIndex) => new()
            {
                BoneIndex0 = checked((uint)boneIndex),
                BoneIndex1 = checked((uint)boneIndex),
                BoneIndex2 = checked((uint)boneIndex),
                BoneIndex3 = checked((uint)boneIndex),
                Weight0 = 1f,
                Weight1 = 0f,
                Weight2 = 0f,
                Weight3 = 0f
            };

            public static SkinWeightData Interpolate(SkinWeightData a, SkinWeightData b,
                SkinWeightData c, Vector3 barycentric, uint[] scratchIndices,
                float[] scratchWeights)
            {
                int influenceCount = 0;
                AddInfluences(a, Mathf.Max(0f, barycentric.x), scratchIndices, scratchWeights,
                    ref influenceCount);
                AddInfluences(b, Mathf.Max(0f, barycentric.y), scratchIndices, scratchWeights,
                    ref influenceCount);
                AddInfluences(c, Mathf.Max(0f, barycentric.z), scratchIndices, scratchWeights,
                    ref influenceCount);

                var result = new SkinWeightData();
                SelectStrongest(ref result.BoneIndex0, ref result.Weight0, scratchIndices,
                    scratchWeights, influenceCount);
                SelectStrongest(ref result.BoneIndex1, ref result.Weight1, scratchIndices,
                    scratchWeights, influenceCount);
                SelectStrongest(ref result.BoneIndex2, ref result.Weight2, scratchIndices,
                    scratchWeights, influenceCount);
                SelectStrongest(ref result.BoneIndex3, ref result.Weight3, scratchIndices,
                    scratchWeights, influenceCount);

                float total = result.Weight0 + result.Weight1 + result.Weight2 + result.Weight3;
                if (total <= 1e-8f)
                    return Rigid(0);
                float inverseTotal = 1f / total;
                result.Weight0 *= inverseTotal;
                result.Weight1 *= inverseTotal;
                result.Weight2 *= inverseTotal;
                result.Weight3 *= inverseTotal;
                return result;
            }

            static void AddInfluences(SkinWeightData source, float barycentricWeight,
                uint[] indices, float[] weights, ref int count)
            {
                AddInfluence(source.BoneIndex0, source.Weight0 * barycentricWeight,
                    indices, weights, ref count);
                AddInfluence(source.BoneIndex1, source.Weight1 * barycentricWeight,
                    indices, weights, ref count);
                AddInfluence(source.BoneIndex2, source.Weight2 * barycentricWeight,
                    indices, weights, ref count);
                AddInfluence(source.BoneIndex3, source.Weight3 * barycentricWeight,
                    indices, weights, ref count);
            }

            static void AddInfluence(uint boneIndex, float weight, uint[] indices,
                float[] weights, ref int count)
            {
                if (weight <= 1e-8f)
                    return;
                for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i)
                {
                    if (indices[i] != boneIndex)
                        continue;
                    weights[i] += weight;
                    return;
                }
                indices[count] = boneIndex;
                weights[count] = weight;
                count++;
            }

            static void SelectStrongest(ref uint boneIndex, ref float weight, uint[] indices,
                float[] weights, int count)
            {
                int strongest = -1;
                float strongestWeight = -1f;
                for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i)
                {
                    if (weights[i] <= strongestWeight)
                        continue;
                    strongest = i;
                    strongestWeight = weights[i];
                }
                if (strongest < 0)
                {
                    boneIndex = 0;
                    weight = 0f;
                    return;
                }
                boneIndex = indices[strongest];
                weight = strongestWeight;
                weights[strongest] = -1f;
            }

            static bool IsValidBoneIndex(int index, int boneCount) =>
                index >= 0 && index < boneCount;

            public Vector3 TransformPoint(Vector3 point, Matrix4x4[] boneMatrices)
            {
                return BlendMatrix(boneMatrices).MultiplyPoint3x4(point);
            }

            public Matrix4x4 BlendMatrix(Matrix4x4[] boneMatrices)
            {
                Matrix4x4 a = boneMatrices[BoneIndex0];
                Matrix4x4 b = boneMatrices[BoneIndex1];
                Matrix4x4 c = boneMatrices[BoneIndex2];
                Matrix4x4 d = boneMatrices[BoneIndex3];
                return new Matrix4x4(
                    a.GetColumn(0) * Weight0 + b.GetColumn(0) * Weight1 +
                    c.GetColumn(0) * Weight2 + d.GetColumn(0) * Weight3,
                    a.GetColumn(1) * Weight0 + b.GetColumn(1) * Weight1 +
                    c.GetColumn(1) * Weight2 + d.GetColumn(1) * Weight3,
                    a.GetColumn(2) * Weight0 + b.GetColumn(2) * Weight1 +
                    c.GetColumn(2) * Weight2 + d.GetColumn(2) * Weight3,
                    a.GetColumn(3) * Weight0 + b.GetColumn(3) * Weight1 +
                    c.GetColumn(3) * Weight2 + d.GetColumn(3) * Weight3);
            }
        }

        readonly struct ProxyTriangle
        {
            public readonly int A;
            public readonly int B;
            public readonly int C;

            public ProxyTriangle(int a, int b, int c)
            {
                A = a;
                B = b;
                C = c;
            }
        }

        sealed class TriangleBvh
        {
            const int k_leafSize = 8;

            struct Node
            {
                public Vector3 Min;
                public Vector3 Max;
                public int Left;
                public int Right;
                public int Start;
                public int Count;
            }

            readonly Vector3[] m_vertices;
            readonly ProxyTriangle[] m_triangles;
            readonly int[] m_order;
            readonly Node[] m_nodes;
            int m_nodeCount;
            readonly int m_root;

            public TriangleBvh(Vector3[] vertices, ProxyTriangle[] triangles)
            {
                m_vertices = vertices ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(vertices));
                m_triangles = triangles ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(triangles));
                if (triangles.Length == 0)
                    throw new ArgumentException("The proxy hierarchy contains no triangles.",
                        nameof(triangles));
                m_order = new int[triangles.Length];
                m_nodes = new Node[checked(triangles.Length * 2)];
                for (int i = 0; i < m_order.Length; ++i)
                    m_order[i] = i;
                m_root = Build(0, triangles.Length);
            }

            int Build(int start, int count)
            {
                Vector3 min = new(float.PositiveInfinity, float.PositiveInfinity,
                    float.PositiveInfinity);
                Vector3 max = new(float.NegativeInfinity, float.NegativeInfinity,
                    float.NegativeInfinity);
                Vector3 centroidMin = min;
                Vector3 centroidMax = max;
                for (int i = start; i < start + count; ++i)
                {
                    ProxyTriangle triangle = m_triangles[m_order[i]];
                    Vector3 a = m_vertices[triangle.A];
                    Vector3 b = m_vertices[triangle.B];
                    Vector3 c = m_vertices[triangle.C];
                    min = Vector3.Min(min, Vector3.Min(a, Vector3.Min(b, c)));
                    max = Vector3.Max(max, Vector3.Max(a, Vector3.Max(b, c)));
                    Vector3 centroid = (a + b + c) / 3f;
                    centroidMin = Vector3.Min(centroidMin, centroid);
                    centroidMax = Vector3.Max(centroidMax, centroid);
                }

                int nodeIndex = m_nodeCount++;
                if (count <= k_leafSize)
                {
                    m_nodes[nodeIndex] = new Node
                    {
                        Min = min, Max = max, Left = -1, Right = -1,
                        Start = start, Count = count
                    };
                    return nodeIndex;
                }

                Vector3 extent = centroidMax - centroidMin;
                int axis = extent.x >= extent.y && extent.x >= extent.z
                    ? 0
                    : extent.y >= extent.z ? 1 : 2;
                int leftCount = count / 2;
                int median = start + leftCount;
                if (extent[axis] > 1e-10f)
                    Select(start, start + count - 1, median, axis);
                int left = Build(start, leftCount);
                int right = Build(median, count - leftCount);
                m_nodes[nodeIndex] = new Node
                {
                    Min = min, Max = max, Left = left, Right = right,
                    Start = 0, Count = 0
                };
                return nodeIndex;
            }

            void Select(int left, int right, int target, int axis)
            {
                while (left < right)
                {
                    int pivot = Partition(left, right, (left + right) >> 1, axis);
                    if (pivot == target)
                        return;
                    if (target < pivot)
                        right = pivot - 1;
                    else
                        left = pivot + 1;
                }
            }

            int Partition(int left, int right, int pivot, int axis)
            {
                float pivotValue = CentroidAxis(m_order[pivot], axis);
                Swap(pivot, right);
                int store = left;
                for (int i = left; i < right; ++i)
                {
                    if (CentroidAxis(m_order[i], axis) >= pivotValue)
                        continue;
                    Swap(store, i);
                    store++;
                }
                Swap(store, right);
                return store;
            }

            float CentroidAxis(int triangleIndex, int axis)
            {
                ProxyTriangle triangle = m_triangles[triangleIndex];
                return (m_vertices[triangle.A][axis] + m_vertices[triangle.B][axis] +
                        m_vertices[triangle.C][axis]) / 3f;
            }

            void Swap(int a, int b) =>
                (m_order[a], m_order[b]) = (m_order[b], m_order[a]);

            public ProxyTriangle FindNearest(Vector3 point, out Vector3 barycentric)
            {
                int nearestTriangle = -1;
                float nearestDistance = float.PositiveInfinity;
                barycentric = new Vector3(1f, 0f, 0f);
                Search(m_root, point, ref nearestTriangle, ref nearestDistance,
                    ref barycentric);
                if (nearestTriangle < 0)
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("Could not find a proxy surface triangle.");
                return m_triangles[nearestTriangle];
            }

            void Search(int nodeIndex, Vector3 point, ref int nearestTriangle,
                ref float nearestDistance, ref Vector3 nearestBarycentric)
            {
                Node node = m_nodes[nodeIndex];
                if (DistanceToBoundsSquared(point, node.Min, node.Max) > nearestDistance)
                    return;

                if (node.Count > 0)
                {
                    for (int i = node.Start; i < node.Start + node.Count; ++i)
                    {
                        int triangleIndex = m_order[i];
                        ProxyTriangle triangle = m_triangles[triangleIndex];
                        float distance = ClosestBarycentric(point, m_vertices[triangle.A],
                            m_vertices[triangle.B], m_vertices[triangle.C],
                            out Vector3 candidateBarycentric);
                        if (distance >= nearestDistance)
                            continue;
                        nearestDistance = distance;
                        nearestTriangle = triangleIndex;
                        nearestBarycentric = candidateBarycentric;
                    }
                    return;
                }

                Node left = m_nodes[node.Left];
                Node right = m_nodes[node.Right];
                float leftDistance = DistanceToBoundsSquared(point, left.Min, left.Max);
                float rightDistance = DistanceToBoundsSquared(point, right.Min, right.Max);
                if (leftDistance <= rightDistance)
                {
                    if (leftDistance <= nearestDistance)
                        Search(node.Left, point, ref nearestTriangle, ref nearestDistance,
                            ref nearestBarycentric);
                    if (rightDistance <= nearestDistance)
                        Search(node.Right, point, ref nearestTriangle, ref nearestDistance,
                            ref nearestBarycentric);
                }
                else
                {
                    if (rightDistance <= nearestDistance)
                        Search(node.Right, point, ref nearestTriangle, ref nearestDistance,
                            ref nearestBarycentric);
                    if (leftDistance <= nearestDistance)
                        Search(node.Left, point, ref nearestTriangle, ref nearestDistance,
                            ref nearestBarycentric);
                }
            }

            static float DistanceToBoundsSquared(Vector3 point, Vector3 min, Vector3 max)
            {
                float dx = point.x < min.x ? min.x - point.x : point.x > max.x ? point.x - max.x : 0f;
                float dy = point.y < min.y ? min.y - point.y : point.y > max.y ? point.y - max.y : 0f;
                float dz = point.z < min.z ? min.z - point.z : point.z > max.z ? point.z - max.z : 0f;
                return dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz;
            }

            static float ClosestBarycentric(Vector3 point, Vector3 a, Vector3 b, Vector3 c,
                out Vector3 barycentric)
            {
                Vector3 ab = b - a;
                Vector3 ac = c - a;
                if (Vector3.Cross(ab, ac).sqrMagnitude <= 1e-16f)
                    return ClosestDegenerate(point, a, b, c, out barycentric);

                Vector3 ap = point - a;
                float d1 = Vector3.Dot(ab, ap);
                float d2 = Vector3.Dot(ac, ap);
                if (d1 <= 0f && d2 <= 0f)
                {
                    barycentric = new Vector3(1f, 0f, 0f);
                    return ap.sqrMagnitude;
                }

                Vector3 bp = point - b;
                float d3 = Vector3.Dot(ab, bp);
                float d4 = Vector3.Dot(ac, bp);
                if (d3 >= 0f && d4 <= d3)
                {
                    barycentric = new Vector3(0f, 1f, 0f);
                    return bp.sqrMagnitude;
                }

                float vc = d1 * d4 - d3 * d2;
                if (vc <= 0f && d1 >= 0f && d3 <= 0f)
                {
                    float v = d1 / (d1 - d3);
                    barycentric = new Vector3(1f - v, v, 0f);
                    return (point - (a + v * ab)).sqrMagnitude;
                }

                Vector3 cp = point - c;
                float d5 = Vector3.Dot(ab, cp);
                float d6 = Vector3.Dot(ac, cp);
                if (d6 >= 0f && d5 <= d6)
                {
                    barycentric = new Vector3(0f, 0f, 1f);
                    return cp.sqrMagnitude;
                }

                float vb = d5 * d2 - d1 * d6;
                if (vb <= 0f && d2 >= 0f && d6 <= 0f)
                {
                    float w = d2 / (d2 - d6);
                    barycentric = new Vector3(1f - w, 0f, w);
                    return (point - (a + w * ac)).sqrMagnitude;
                }

                float va = d3 * d6 - d5 * d4;
                if (va <= 0f && d4 - d3 >= 0f && d5 - d6 >= 0f)
                {
                    float w = (d4 - d3) / ((d4 - d3) + (d5 - d6));
                    barycentric = new Vector3(0f, 1f - w, w);
                    return (point - (b + w * (c - b))).sqrMagnitude;
                }

                float inverseDenominator = 1f / (va + vb + vc);
                float faceV = vb * inverseDenominator;
                float faceW = vc * inverseDenominator;
                barycentric = new Vector3(1f - faceV - faceW, faceV, faceW);
                Vector3 closest = a * barycentric.x + b * barycentric.y + c * barycentric.z;
                return (point - closest).sqrMagnitude;
            }

            static float ClosestDegenerate(Vector3 point, Vector3 a, Vector3 b, Vector3 c,
                out Vector3 barycentric)
            {
                float ab = ClosestSegment(point, a, b, out float tAb);
                float bc = ClosestSegment(point, b, c, out float tBc);
                float ca = ClosestSegment(point, c, a, out float tCa);
                if (ab <= bc && ab <= ca)
                {
                    barycentric = new Vector3(1f - tAb, tAb, 0f);
                    return ab;
                }
                if (bc <= ca)
                {
                    barycentric = new Vector3(0f, 1f - tBc, tBc);
                    return bc;
                }
                barycentric = new Vector3(tCa, 0f, 1f - tCa);
                return ca;
            }

            static float ClosestSegment(Vector3 point, Vector3 a, Vector3 b, out float t)
            {
                Vector3 segment = b - a;
                float denominator = segment.sqrMagnitude;
                t = denominator > 1e-16f
                    ? Mathf.Clamp01(Vector3.Dot(point - a, segment) / denominator)
                    : 0f;
                return (point - (a + t * segment)).sqrMagnitude;
            }
        }

        GsplatRenderer m_splatRenderer;
        GsplatAsset m_boundAsset;
        Transform m_boundProxyRoot;
        Transform m_resolvedProxyRoot;
        Mesh[] m_boundMeshes;
        Renderer[] m_proxyMeshRenderers = Array.Empty<Renderer>();
        Transform[] m_paletteBones;
        Matrix4x4[] m_paletteBindTransforms;
        SkinWeightData[] m_skinWeights;
        Vector4[] m_referenceInverseRows;
        Matrix4x4[] m_boneMatrices;
        GraphicsBuffer m_skinWeightBuffer;
        GraphicsBuffer m_referenceInverseBuffer;
        GraphicsBuffer m_boneMatrixBuffer;
        Texture2D m_skinIndexTexture;
        Texture2D m_skinWeightTexture;
        Texture2D m_referenceInverseTexture;
        Texture2D m_boneMatrixTexture;
        Vector4[] m_boneMatrixRows;
        bool m_usesVertexTextures;
        bool m_initializationFailed;
        Renderer[] m_hiddenRenderers = Array.Empty<Renderer>();
        bool[] m_previousForceRenderingOff = Array.Empty<bool>();
        readonly List<SkinningValidationGroup> m_validationGroups = new();
        int m_validationFramesRemaining;

        const int k_validationDelayFrames = 10;
        const int k_validationSamplesPerRenderer = 64;

        sealed class SkinningValidationGroup
        {
            public Renderer Renderer;
            public int[] VertexIndices;
            public Vector3[] SourcePositions;
            public Vector3[] ReferencePositions;
            public SkinWeightData[] SkinWeights;
            public Matrix4x4[] ReferenceInverses;
        }

        internal override GraphicsBuffer SkinWeightBuffer => m_skinWeightBuffer;
        internal override GraphicsBuffer ReferenceInverseBuffer => m_referenceInverseBuffer;
        internal override GraphicsBuffer BoneRowBuffer => m_boneMatrixBuffer;
        internal override Texture2D SkinIndexTexture => m_skinIndexTexture;
        internal override Texture2D SkinWeightTexture => m_skinWeightTexture;
        internal override Texture2D ReferenceInverseTexture => m_referenceInverseTexture;
        internal override Texture2D BoneMatrixTexture => m_boneMatrixTexture;
        internal override bool UsesVertexTextures => m_usesVertexTextures;
        public bool IsReady => m_skinWeights != null && m_referenceInverseRows != null &&
                               m_boneMatrices != null &&
                               (m_usesVertexTextures
                                   ? m_skinIndexTexture && m_skinWeightTexture &&
                                     m_referenceInverseTexture && m_boneMatrixTexture
                                   : m_skinWeightBuffer != null &&
                                     m_referenceInverseBuffer != null &&
                                     m_boneMatrixBuffer != null);
        internal override bool IsActivelyAnimating => IsReady && isActiveAndEnabled &&
                                                      (Application.isPlaying || AnimateInEditMode);

        void OnEnable()
        {
            m_splatRenderer = GetComponent<GsplatRenderer>();
            ResolveProxyHierarchy();
            ApplyProxyVisibility();
            if (Application.isPlaying)
                TryInitialize();
        }

        void Update()
        {
            // Generate deterministic bind-pose bindings before Unity's animation update. Binding
            // must not depend on whichever animation-preview pose happens to be active here.
            if (Application.isPlaying)
            {
                if (!IsReady)
                    TryInitialize();
            }
            else if (AnimateInEditMode)
                RefreshAnimation();
        }

        void LateUpdate()
        {
            if (Application.isPlaying)
                RefreshAnimation();
        }

        void OnDisable()
        {
            RestoreProxyVisibility();
            ReleaseResources();
            m_splatRenderer?.NotifySkinningChanged();
        }

        void OnDestroy()
        {
            RestoreProxyVisibility();
            ReleaseResources();
        }

        void OnValidate()
        {
            BoundsPadding = Mathf.Max(0f, BoundsPadding);
            m_initializationFailed = false;
            m_splatRenderer ??= GetComponent<GsplatRenderer>();
            ResolveProxyHierarchy(true);
            ApplyProxyVisibility();
            if (IsReady && !BindingsMatchCurrentHierarchy())
                ReleaseResources();
        }

        [ContextMenu("Rebuild Gaussian Skin Bindings")]
        public void RebuildBindings()
        {
            m_initializationFailed = false;
            ReleaseResources();
            ResolveProxyHierarchy(true);
            ApplyProxyVisibility();
            TryInitialize();
            RefreshAnimation();
        }

        void RefreshAnimation()
        {
            if (!TryInitialize())
                return;
            UpdateBoneMatrices();
            UploadBoneMatrices();
#if UNITY_EDITOR
            ValidateSkinningWhenReady();
#endif
            m_splatRenderer.NotifySkinningChanged();
        }

        bool TryInitialize()
        {
            m_splatRenderer ??= GetComponent<GsplatRenderer>();
            ResolveProxyHierarchy();
            if (IsReady)
            {
                if (BindingsMatchCurrentHierarchy())
                    return true;
                ReleaseResources();
            }
            if (m_initializationFailed)
                return false;

            if (!m_splatRenderer || !m_splatRenderer.GsplatAsset || !ProxyRoot ||
                m_proxyMeshRenderers.Length == 0)
                return false;

            try
            {
                Stopwatch stopwatch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                Vector3[] splatCenters = GetSplatCenters(m_splatRenderer.GsplatAsset);
                if (splatCenters.Length == 0)
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("The Gaussian asset contains no splats to bind.");

                var proxyVertices = new List<Vector3>();
                var proxyWeights = new List<SkinWeightData>();
                var proxyTriangles = new List<ProxyTriangle>();
                var paletteBones = new List<Transform> { null };
                var paletteBindTransforms = new List<Matrix4x4> { Matrix4x4.identity };
                var validationGroups = new List<SkinningValidationGroup>();
                var boundMeshes = new Mesh[m_proxyMeshRenderers.Length];
                Matrix4x4 splatToWorld = m_splatRenderer.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
                Matrix4x4 worldToSplat = m_splatRenderer.transform.worldToLocalMatrix;

                for (int rendererIndex = 0; rendererIndex < m_proxyMeshRenderers.Length; ++rendererIndex)
                {
                    Renderer proxy = m_proxyMeshRenderers[rendererIndex];
                    Mesh mesh = GetProxyMesh(proxy);
                    if (!mesh)
                        throw new InvalidOperationException($"Proxy renderer '{proxy.name}' has no mesh.");
                    if (!mesh.isReadable)
                        throw new InvalidOperationException(
                            $"Enable Read/Write on proxy mesh '{mesh.name}' so Gaussian skin bindings can be generated.");

                    boundMeshes[rendererIndex] = mesh;
                    Vector3[] meshVertices = mesh.vertices;
                    if (meshVertices.Length == 0)
                        continue;

                    var skinnedProxy = proxy as SkinnedMeshRenderer;
                    Transform[] bones = skinnedProxy ? skinnedProxy.bones : null;
                    Matrix4x4[] bindPoses = mesh.bindposes;
                    BoneWeight[] vertexWeights = mesh.boneWeights;
                    bool rigid = !skinnedProxy || bones == null || bones.Length == 0;
                    if (!rigid && (bindPoses.Length != bones.Length ||
                                   vertexWeights.Length != meshVertices.Length))
                        throw new InvalidOperationException(
                            $"Proxy mesh '{mesh.name}' must provide matching bind poses, bones, and four-bone vertex weights.");

                    Matrix4x4 splatToMesh = proxy.transform.worldToLocalMatrix * splatToWorld;
                    Matrix4x4 meshToSplat = worldToSplat * proxy.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
                    int paletteOffset = paletteBones.Count;
                    if (rigid)
                    {
                        paletteBones.Add(proxy.transform);
                        paletteBindTransforms.Add(splatToMesh);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        for (int boneIndex = 0; boneIndex < bones.Length; ++boneIndex)
                        {
                            paletteBones.Add(bones[boneIndex]);
                            paletteBindTransforms.Add(bindPoses[boneIndex] * splatToMesh);
                        }
                    }

                    int vertexOffset = proxyVertices.Count;
                    for (int vertexIndex = 0; vertexIndex < meshVertices.Length; ++vertexIndex)
                    {
                        proxyVertices.Add(meshToSplat.MultiplyPoint3x4(meshVertices[vertexIndex]));
                        proxyWeights.Add(rigid
                            ? SkinWeightData.Rigid(paletteOffset)
                            : new SkinWeightData(vertexWeights[vertexIndex], bones.Length,
                                paletteOffset));
                    }

                    if (ValidateSkinningAgainstProxy)
                    {
                        int sampleCount = Mathf.Min(k_validationSamplesPerRenderer,
                            meshVertices.Length);
                        var sampleIndices = new int[sampleCount];
                        var sampleSources = new Vector3[sampleCount];
                        var sampleReferences = new Vector3[sampleCount];
                        var sampleWeights = new SkinWeightData[sampleCount];
                        for (int sampleIndex = 0; sampleIndex < sampleCount; ++sampleIndex)
                        {
                            int vertexIndex = sampleCount == 1
                                ? 0
                                : Mathf.RoundToInt(sampleIndex * (meshVertices.Length - 1f) /
                                                   (sampleCount - 1f));
                            sampleIndices[sampleIndex] = vertexIndex;
                            sampleSources[sampleIndex] = meshToSplat.MultiplyPoint3x4(
                                meshVertices[vertexIndex]);
                            sampleReferences[sampleIndex] = sampleSources[sampleIndex];
                            sampleWeights[sampleIndex] = proxyWeights[vertexOffset + vertexIndex];
                        }
                        validationGroups.Add(new SkinningValidationGroup
                        {
                            Renderer = proxy,
                            VertexIndices = sampleIndices,
                            SourcePositions = sampleSources,
                            ReferencePositions = sampleReferences,
                            SkinWeights = sampleWeights
                        });
                    }

                    int[] meshTriangles = mesh.triangles;
                    if (meshTriangles.Length % 3 != 0)
                        throw new InvalidOperationException(
                            $"Proxy mesh '{mesh.name}' contains an invalid triangle index buffer.");
                    for (int triangleIndex = 0; triangleIndex < meshTriangles.Length;
                         triangleIndex += 3)
                    {
                        int a = meshTriangles[triangleIndex];
                        int b = meshTriangles[triangleIndex + 1];
                        int c = meshTriangles[triangleIndex + 2];
                        if ((uint)a >= (uint)meshVertices.Length ||
                            (uint)b >= (uint)meshVertices.Length ||
                            (uint)c >= (uint)meshVertices.Length)
                            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                                $"Proxy mesh '{mesh.name}' contains an out-of-range triangle index.");
                        proxyTriangles.Add(new ProxyTriangle(vertexOffset + a,
                            vertexOffset + b, vertexOffset + c));
                    }
                }

                if (proxyVertices.Count == 0)
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("The proxy hierarchy contains no bindable mesh vertices.");
                if (proxyTriangles.Count == 0)
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("The proxy hierarchy contains no bindable triangles.");
                Vector3[] vertices = proxyVertices.ToArray();
                SkinWeightData[] vertexSkinWeights = proxyWeights.ToArray();
                ProxyTriangle[] triangles = proxyTriangles.ToArray();
                var surface = new TriangleBvh(vertices, triangles);
                var bindings = new SkinWeightData[splatCenters.Length];
                var scratchIndices = new uint[12];
                var scratchWeights = new float[12];
                for (int i = 0; i < splatCenters.Length; ++i)
                {
                    ProxyTriangle triangle = surface.FindNearest(splatCenters[i],
                        out Vector3 barycentric);
                    bindings[i] = SkinWeightData.Interpolate(
                        vertexSkinWeights[triangle.A], vertexSkinWeights[triangle.B],
                        vertexSkinWeights[triangle.C], barycentric, scratchIndices,
                        scratchWeights);
                }

                m_skinWeights = bindings;
                m_paletteBones = paletteBones.ToArray();
                m_paletteBindTransforms = paletteBindTransforms.ToArray();
                m_boneMatrices = new Matrix4x4[m_paletteBones.Length];
                m_boundAsset = m_splatRenderer.GsplatAsset;
                m_boundProxyRoot = ProxyRoot;
                m_boundMeshes = boundMeshes;
                UpdateAbsoluteBoneMatrices();
                CreateReferenceInverseRows(bindings);
                PrepareSkinningValidation(validationGroups);
                m_usesVertexTextures = GsplatUtils.UseVertexDataTextures;
                if (m_usesVertexTextures)
                    CreateSkinTextures(bindings, m_paletteBones.Length);
                else
                    CreateSkinBuffers(bindings, m_paletteBones.Length);
                UploadBoneMatrices();
                stopwatch.Stop();
                Debug.Log(
                    $"[GsplatProxyAnimator] Bound {bindings.Length:N0} splats to {vertices.Length:N0} " +
                    $"vertices/{triangles.Length:N0} triangles across " +
                    $"{m_proxyMeshRenderers.Length} proxy meshes and " +
                    $"{m_paletteBones.Length - 1} bone slots using deterministic bind-pose skinning " +
                    $"in {stopwatch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds:F2}s.", this);
                GsplatSorter.Instance.MarkGlobalBuffersDirty();
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception exception)
            {
                ReleaseResources();
                Fail(exception.Message);
                Debug.LogException(exception, this);
                return false;
            }
        }

        void UpdateBoneMatrices()
        {
            UpdateAbsoluteBoneMatrices();
        }

        void CreateReferenceInverseRows(SkinWeightData[] bindings)
        {
            m_referenceInverseRows = new Vector4[checked(bindings.Length * 3)];
            for (int i = 0; i < bindings.Length; ++i)
            {
                int offset = i * 3;
                m_referenceInverseRows[offset] = new Vector4(1f, 0f, 0f, 0f);
                m_referenceInverseRows[offset + 1] = new Vector4(0f, 1f, 0f, 0f);
                m_referenceInverseRows[offset + 2] = new Vector4(0f, 0f, 1f, 0f);
            }
        }

        void PrepareSkinningValidation(List<SkinningValidationGroup> groups)
        {
            m_validationGroups.Clear();
            m_validationFramesRemaining = 0;
            if (!ValidateSkinningAgainstProxy)
                return;

            foreach (SkinningValidationGroup group in groups)
            {
                group.ReferenceInverses = new Matrix4x4[group.SkinWeights.Length];
                for (int i = 0; i < group.SkinWeights.Length; ++i)
                    group.ReferenceInverses[i] = Matrix4x4.identity;
                m_validationGroups.Add(group);
            }
            m_validationFramesRemaining = k_validationDelayFrames;
        }

#if UNITY_EDITOR
        void ValidateSkinningWhenReady()
        {
            if (!ValidateSkinningAgainstProxy || m_validationFramesRemaining <= 0 ||
                m_validationGroups.Count == 0)
                return;
            if (--m_validationFramesRemaining > 0)
                return;

            double bindErrorTotal = 0.0;
            double animatedErrorTotal = 0.0;
            float bindErrorMax = 0f;
            float animatedErrorMax = 0f;
            int sampleCount = 0;
            Matrix4x4 splatToWorld = m_splatRenderer.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
            Matrix4x4 worldToSplat = m_splatRenderer.transform.worldToLocalMatrix;

            foreach (SkinningValidationGroup group in m_validationGroups)
            {
                Mesh sharedMesh = GetProxyMesh(group.Renderer);
                if (!sharedMesh)
                    continue;
                Vector3[] currentVertices = GetCurrentProxyVertices(group.Renderer, sharedMesh);
                Matrix4x4 meshToSplat = worldToSplat * group.Renderer.transform.localToWorldMatrix;
                for (int i = 0; i < group.VertexIndices.Length; ++i)
                {
                    Matrix4x4 current = group.SkinWeights[i].BlendMatrix(m_boneMatrices);
